## AD-2

Acoustic fixed-chain processor; partial, checked 2026-10-18. Included for distinct resonance, ambience and feedback processing, not its preset/mode count. [Manufacturer](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ad-2/).

<!-- comparison evidence begins -->

| Field | Finding / qualification |
|---|---|
| Expression | No built-in pedal/toe or external EXP socket in published connector inventory. |
| K/F/B | **3/1/0**, manufacturer text controls; visual audit pending. |
| Display | CHECK/battery indicator; screen diagonal/color/touch not specified. |
| Processing / categories | Acoustic Resonance, Ambience reverb and notch filtering; fixed controls, not a selectable multi-model library. Numeric simultaneous-block count unpublished. |
| Memory | Factory/user/total preset memory unverified. |
| Synth / acoustic | Acoustic-electric resonance restoration, optimized for piezo pickups. [Roland distinguishes AD-2 from AC-3](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404893445403): AD-2 restores acoustic input tone; AC-3 converts electric guitar tone. Do not classify AD-2 as an electric-to-acoustic simulator. Synth remains unverified. |
| Dimensions / mass | **73W×129D×59H mm;420g including battery**. |
| I/O | INPUT,OUTPUT ¼-inch; balanced LINE OUT ¼-inch TRS;DC IN. USB/MIDI absent from published inventory. |
| Power | 9V alkaline6LR61 or optional PSA;65mA;approximately5h battery,condition-dependent. PSA-family voltage/polarity cross-check below; USB power unverified. |
| Editor / platforms | Editor,drivers and software platforms unverified. |
| IR / NAM / capture | Unverified. |
| Practical features | Buffered bypass,mute;LINE OUT can omit Ambience independently. Looper/drums/scenes unverified. |
| IDR price | [Indexed Shopee offer](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-AD-2-AD2-Acoustic-Preamp-Guitar-Acoustic-Effect-i.16879370.651392518): **Rp2,150,500**,Seven Stereo Music & Audio Store,Bandung;seller16879370/item651392518. Checked2026-10-18;index crawled roughly2months earlier,delivery dates19–22August. New/used condition,live stock,bundle,warranty andcheckout voucher unverified;late-delivery compensation isnot an immediate price discount. No qualified used ask. |
| I/O / routing and mute | [Manufacturer Portuguese manual,revision02,©2016](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/AD-2_M_ptb02_W.pdf),translated text,checked2026-10-18: holdfootswitch≥2s tomute;CHECKflashes,pressagain tounmute. INPUT insertion powersunit;unplug whenunused. LINE OUT isbalancedmono,explicitly notheadphones. Holdpedal duringpower-on,adjustAMBIENCE,release tofinish: reverb canfeedbothoutputs orOUTPUTonly. Exactknobpositions/pinout needvisualverification. |
| Power / source priority | Same manual: PSAadapter takespriority overinstalledbattery;retainingbattery allowscontinuedoperation ifadapter disconnects. This source-priority passage alone does not establish USB power or DC plug polarity; see the separate PSA-family cross-check below. |
| Acoustic / pickup compatibility | [Exact-model Roland support](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404885412763), updated 2025-01-24: non-piezo pickups may yield a weaker effect. [Simulator-input qualification](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404885409691): feeding AC-3 or similar simulated acoustic output may not achieve the intended result; guitar, pickup and tone settings can make processing too weak or excessive. Translated Japanese manufacturer text, checked 2026-10-18. |
| I/O / output voicing | [Roland output FAQ](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404885409179), updated 2025-01-24: OUTPUT is voiced primarily for acoustic guitar amplifiers and carries Ambience, Notch and Acoustic Resonance. LINE OUT targets PA/stage amplification and can omit Ambience for mixer-side reverb; these are not simply two identical sockets. |
| Power / named adapter cross-check | AD-2 explicitly specifies optional PSA-series power. [Roland PSA-S specifications](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/201953449-BOSS-PSA-S-Adaptor-Specifications), updated2026-06-16, specify **regulated9V DC,0.5A,center-negative**, with100–240V50/60Hz mains input. Family-member cross-check, not inspection of the actual regional/replacement supply or connector diameter. **500mA is adapter capacity;65mA is AD-2's published draw.** Confirm regional plug compatibility. |
| Audio / electrical specifications | [AD-2 own specifications](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ad-2/): nominal input and both outputs **−20dBu**; input **10MΩ**. OUTPUT impedance **1kΩ**,recommended load≥10kΩ; LINE OUT impedance **600Ω**,recommended load≥600Ω. Output impedance and connected load are separate ratings; no measured results claimed. |

### AD-2 — expression screening and published specification conflict

Checked2026-10-28 against the [original English e01 manual](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/AD-2_e01_W.pdf),©2016,and [current product specifications](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ad-2/). Published connector inventory has no analog EXP;LINE OUT explicitly cannot serve as headphones. Single pedal switch operates effects/mute,not documented preset down/up. Acoustic-electric input is documented;ordinary electric-to-acoustic simulation is not established. 73W×129Dmm fits preference,without meeting direct-expression requirement.

**Revision/source conflict retained:**e01 specifies55mA,approximately6h alkaline life and450g including battery;current product lists65mA,approximately5h and420g including battery. Do not average these or infer which revision a seller supplies. Direct analog volume placement/range/recall are inapplicable;tail/loop attenuation remains unverified. Existing buffered-bypass andmute qualifications remain.

## AD-8

Acoustic-guitar multi-effects: body modeling, EQ, reverb and feedback suppression; distinct from AD-10 and single-function acoustic simulators. Checked 2026-10-17; partial. No documented bass/vocal membership adopted.

<!-- comparison evidence begins -->

| Field | Verified finding / limitation |
|---|---|
| Expression | No built-in expression pedal or external EXP socket in the [official manual inventory][AD8-M]; no toe switch listed. |
| Controls | **10 rotary / 3 footswitches / 4 buttons**, counted from [BOSS Japan’s control list][AD8-J]. Stereo/mono and ground-lift switches are separate. Text-derived inventory, not visual inspection. |
| Display | **Seven-segment LED**; published diagonal, emission color and touch specification unverified. [AD8-S] |
| Processing / category choices | **Six acoustic body models**, four-band EQ, acoustic reverb and automatic anti-feedback. Explicit maximum simultaneous count remains unverified; the manual’s reverb choices are documented below. [AD8-S] |
| Factory / user memory | **Four stored setups plus live Manual mode**, not five stored presets. [AD8-J] Manual pp20/22 identifies four factory-filled, rewritable memories for strumming, fingerstyle, flat-pick playing and blues. Reset overwrites all four; no extra factory bank. [AD8-M] |
| Synth / acoustic | Piezo-acoustic body/microphone modeling, not proof of electric-to-acoustic conversion. Synth engine unverified. [AD8-S] |
| Dimensions / mass | **250 W × 180 D × 60 H mm; 1.9 kg including batteries**, manual pp25–26. [AD8-M] **Conflict:** Japan lists **250 × 190 × 75 mm**, same mass. [AD8-J] Large under either published width; reason for regional difference unestablished. |
| I/O | Quarter-inch INPUT; quarter-inch stereo TRS PHONES; quarter-inch electric-guitar-amp output; unbalanced quarter-inch A/mono and B; balanced XLR A/B; PSA DC input. XLRs are outputs, not microphone inputs. [AD8-S] |
| I/O / impedance conflict | Manual p25 gives **4.7 MΩ input**, regional web specification **20 MΩ**. Preserve both; no revision explanation verified. [AD8-M], [AD8-S] |
| Power / batteries / USB | **9 V DC, 120 mA in manual/current regional pages; six AA or PSA adapter**. Historical170mA and indexed2011 200mA conflicts documented below. Manual rates carbon batteries about **9 h**, alkaline **23 h**, conditions dependent. Polarity unresolved. No USB connector in the complete port list; USB-powered operation not supported by that inventory. [AD8-M], [AD8-S] |
| Editor / USB | No full editor platform established; no USB or MIDI connector listed. [AD8-S] |
| IR / NAM / capture | User IR, native/converted NAM and proprietary capture loading unverified; COSM body models do not establish user imports. |
| Looper / drums / live | Tuner with mute, reference **438–445 Hz**, accuracy ±3 cents; balanced stereo DI with ground lift. Looper/drums unverified. [AD8-S] |

### AD-8 — manual processing and stage behavior, 2026-10-17

[Own ©2003 manual][AD8-M], printed pp9,13–20,22; text inspection only.

| Field | Verified behavior / limit |
|---|---|
| Processing / reverb and EQ | **Three reverb alternatives: Ambience, Room, Hall**, plus off; not three simultaneous reverbs. Midrange frequency adjusts **200 Hz–1.6 kHz**. |
| Looper / routing / startup | Power-up selects Manual with effects on. Mono output sends identical A/B signals. The output-level knob affects processed sound only; bypass/mute uses its fixed output state. The chosen bypass/mute behavior survives power-off. |
| Live / anti-feedback | One feedback point is detected; attenuation also covers its octave above. Press both memory pedals to detect, hold both for at least two seconds to exit. Settings can be written with a memory, but are not saved in Manual mode. Anti-feedback starts off after power-up. |

### AD-8 — body pairs and historical specification conflict, 2026-10-17

| Field | Verified evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / acoustic model pairs | [Own manual, printed p10][AD8-M]: **1 D-28/U87; 2 000-28/U67; 3 J-45/U67; 4 B-25/U87; 5 D-40/C12; 6 Jose Ramirez/C12**. Guitar makers respectively Martin, Martin, Gibson, Gibson, Guild and Jose Ramirez; microphones Neumann for 1–4, AKG for 5–6. These are six fixed modeled combinations, not six guitars times three independently selectable microphones, physical mic inputs or user captures. |
| Power / historical draw conflict | [Manufacturer 2008 catalogue, indexed AD-8 specifications](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/boss_catalog_2008.pdf) lists **170 mA at 9 V maximum**, alongside20 MΩ input and250 ×180 ×60 mm. This conflicts with the own manual’s **120 mA**, also corroborated by [2012 catalogue](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/boss_catalog_2012.pdf) and [2014 catalogue](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/boss_catalog_2014.pdf). No documented revision or correction reconciles the figures. Do not treat170 mA as an adapter rating. Full2008 PDF exceeded reader size limit; evidence is its indexed model-specific text, not visual inspection. |


### AD-8 — analog levels and power switching

Checked2026-10-18 against [own manual,printed pp13,25–26][AD8-M] and [regional specifications](https://www.boss.info/se/products/ad-8/specifications/). No device operation ordownloads.

| Field | Finding / qualification |
|---|---|
| I/O / nominal levels and impedances | **Nominal input/output−20dBu**;unbalanced/electric-amp outputs **1kΩ**,balancedXLR **600Ω**,phones **33Ω**. Regional recommended load **10kΩ orgreater**;do not reinterpret this as a minimum headphone rating. Nominal levels are not maximum headroom orheadphone power. Manual’s Ω-valued output lines are mislabeled NominalOutputLevel;regional page identifies them as impedances. Existing4.7MΩ/20MΩ input conflict remains. |
| Power / input-jack switching and bundle | Inserting the INPUT plug switches the uniton;removing it switches off. Manufacturer accessories list **sixR6AA batteries**,with **PSA adapter optional**. These are original package contents,not proof ofwhat seller item9817836384 includes. NoUSB/battery-runtime change inferred. |
| I/O / ground lift | NOR/LIFT disconnects **pin1 ground** from inputground whenLIFT isselected. This is an output ground-loop control,not a phantom-power ormicrophone-input switch. Default recommendation isNOR. |


### AD-8 — additional current-draw conflict and retained live settings

| Field | Source evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Power / 2011 catalogue conflict | [Roland’s 2011 catalogue, indexed AD SERIES/AD-8 specification](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/boss_catalog_2011.pdf) lists **200mA**. Full PDF open failed; this is **model-specific indexed text only**, not a visually inspected specification. Preserve alongside **120mA** in the own manual/current regional specifications and **170mA** in the2008 catalogue. Revision or correction unverified; do not average the figures or infer a changed battery runtime. |

The manual’s example saying400Hz conflicts with its explicit range/default; do not expand the supported range from that example. [Own manual pp18,20][AD8-M] |
| Display / memory indicators | Own manualp17 offers one lit selected-memory indicator or a cumulative indicator count. Holding SELECT≥2seconds switches patterns; setting survives power-off. This is LED indication behavior, not screen diagonal/color/touch evidence. [Own manual p17][AD8-M] |
### AD-8 — direct-expression screening (2026-10-28)

[Own manual][AD8-M], printed pp13–14, confirms the complete rear connector inventory has no EXP socket: direct analog expression-volume requirement fails. MEMORY down/up cycles Manual and four memories in opposite directions; PHONES is dedicated. Existing regional footprint conflict remains; both widths exceed preference. Normal electric-guitar tonal suitability remains unverified. Direct-pedal position/range is inapplicable to this inventory; hand OUTPUT LEVEL does not establish pedal assignment or universal attenuation during bypass. No device testing.

Acoustic-electric guitar multi-effects/preamp, checked2026-10-17; partial. [Manufacturer product/specifications][AD10-P] explicitly document multiple effect families; not the dedicated BOSS AC-3 acoustic simulator. Bass/vocal capability and ownership unestablished.

| Field | Verified evidence / gaps |
|---|---|
| Expression | External EV-30,FV-500H/L,EV-5 supported through shared EXP/CTL1,2; alternatively two footswitches. Built-in/toe unverified. |
| Controls | **14 rotary /3 footswitches /19 named buttons**, text-derived inventory below;power separate. Visual corroboration pending. |
| Display | **2-digit7-segment LED**;diagonal/color/touch unverified. |
| Processing / choices | Resonance3 (Natural/Wide/Bright),compressor2 (Soft/Hard),chorus3 (Normal/Mild/Bright),delay3 (Delay/Modulation/Reverse),ambience3 (Short/Mid/Long);off excluded from counts. Four-bandEQ,lowcut,boost,feedback reduction+two notches. Aggregate simultaneous count unestablished. |
| Factory / user memory | **10 stored setups**,plusManual mode;factory-filled count unknown. |
| Synth / acoustic | Acoustic Resonance restores pickup/body response;electric-to-acoustic conversion and synth unestablished. |
| Dimensions / mass | **217W×161D×65Hmm;1.3kg including batteries**. |
| I/O | Two quarter-inch guitar inputs,send/return,stereoXLRouts,quarter-inchL/phones+R/mono,TRSEXP/CTL,USB-B,DC. |
| Power / batteries | **300mA draw;6AA alkaline,about6h**;included adapter. **9VDC** confirmed through model-specific PSA-100 compatibility below;polarity/USB-power unresolved. |
| IR / NAM / captures | User loading unestablished. |
| Editor / platforms | USB audio drivers listed forWindows10/11 andmacOS14+;full editor unverified. |
| Looper / drums / routing | **80s looper;2-in/2-out USB audio**. Outputs selectable with/without effects or separated input channels. Drums/scenes unknown. |

### AD-10 — manual routing and memory qualifications

Checked2026-10-17 against [official English manual][AD10-PDF], file eng04_W,14pages, ©2017 Roland; printed pp2–3,5,9–13.

| Field | Verified behavior / gaps |
|---|---|
| I/O / output routing | XLR andLINE independently select MIX,PRE/POST,orCH. MIX includes USB playback; PRE/POST sends pre-effectsL/processedR; CH sends input2L/input1R. Ambience can feed both output groups,either,or neither. |
| Processing / input2 bypass | Input2 FX BYPASS retains **looper and ambience**. Mono send/return;direct mixing selectable when RETURN is connected. Not a fully dry bypass. |
| Factory / user memory | Ten editable effect/setup memories. Startup defaultsManual,configurable toMemory orMemoryStomp. Per-effect memory/manual selection allows some controls to remain at knob positions across recalls. Factory-filled count unknown. |
| Preset controls / saving | HoldEXIT+MEMORY,confirmMEMORY,choose destination,confirmagain. Recalled knobs use pickup behavior: value changes only after crossing the stored position. |
| USB audio / monitoring | USB direct-outOFF sends unit sound only tocomputer;host-processed return can feed outputs. ON restores local output. Dedicated driver required;not evidence of a full editor orUSB power. |
| Expression / live | External EXP targets output,ambience,orloop level. Three named footswitches: BOOST,DELAY,TUNER&MUTE;rotary/button inventory is documented below. |

[AD10-PDF]: https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/AD-10_eng04_W.pdf

AD-10 draw remains300mA. This establishes compatible supply voltage/capacity,not the identity or mains rating of every regional included adapter. Polarity remains unverified in read text. |

### AD-10 — effect choices and loop operation

Checked2026-10-17 against [eng04_W][AD10-PDF],printed pp7,9–10.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / choices | Named choices above exclude bypass. Ambience selects reverberation length,not documented room/hall algorithms. Category counts do not establish a total simultaneous-block limit. |
| Looper / controls | DELAY+TUNER&MUTE enters loop operation;TUNER&MUTE records,plays,and overdubs. Stop requires two presses;clear requires holding LOOP at least2s while stopped. Sequence defaults record→play→overdub;record→overdub→play is selectable. Playback level50 is unity. Power-off phrase retention,undo/redo,and phrase export remain unverified. |

### AD-10 — feedback, input EQ and output-level options

Checked2026-10-18 against [eng04_W manual pp7–8,10–12][AD10-PDF]. No hardware operation or downloads.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / feedback filters | TwoNOTCH filters specify **A♯1–F7**; pressingbothNOTCHbuttons scansfeedback automatically. Individualnotchdepth1–20; REDUCTION independentlytargets low-frequencyfeedback. Rangeis acontrolspecification,not guaranteedeliminationofallfeedback or extraaudioinputs. |
| I/O / independent input EQ and phase | GUITAR IN1/2 canhaveindependentEQ; withinthatsetup FX LOOPbutton reverses selectedinputphase. EQsubparametersinclude **midQ0.5–16** andnoise-suppressionamount. This isnot documented freechainreordering or twocompleteindependenteffectsengines. |
| I/O / fixed output levels | XLRlevel andheadphonelevel eachselect **fixed0–99 orfollowOUTPUT LEVEL knob**. Thesearecontrolvalues,notdBu. They areseparatefromloopplaybacklevel andUSBinput/outputlevels. |
| Power / automatic shutdown | FactoryAutoOff shutsdownafter **10hours withoutplaying/operation**; disableoptionavailable. This doesnot resolve looppersistence afterpowerloss or replace thesix-houralkalineestimate. |

### AD-10 — system expression-volume screening (2026-10-28)

[Own eng04_W manual][AD10-PDF], printed pp11–12, places pedal-function assignment under System Settings: **EF→ov (OUTPUT LEVEL)**, alternatively AN (ambience) or LP (loop). This establishes a shared assignment, distinct from per-memory stomp-effect settings. Exact volume endpoints/location and held-position recall remain unverified. PHONES/XLR fixed-versus-follow-output settings and output routing remain separate coverage qualifications; no blanket bypass/tail/recorded-loop attenuation claim.

Own p5 confirms foot memory browsing both directions in Memory mode. L/PHONES is a **shared** output, not a separate headphone socket. Acoustic piezo/magnetic pickup blending is documented; ordinary electric-guitar tonal suitability remains unverified. Existing217W×161Dmm exceeds both preferred limits. Controller pinout/resistance remains unverified; no hardware test.

## GM-800

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. This processor qualifies through independent MFX architecture; it requires specialized input hardware for guitar/bass playing. No excluded specialist model is added.

Technical research snapshot: **2026-10-14**. All records remain partial; `?` means unverified, not No. This reclassification preserves earlier sources and uncertainties; it is not a fresh technical verification. Dimensions are W×D×H in millimetres; cables and pedal travel require additional clearance.

<!-- BOSS-GM-800 source begins -->

Included as a multi-instrument synthesizer with multiple independent MFX stages, not merely because it contains multiple synth sounds. Keep its specialized pickup requirements visible rather than treating it as a conventional analog-input guitar processor.

| Field | Preserved comparison evidence |
|---|---|
| Built-in / external expression | No built-in pedal; **2 CTL/EXP sockets**, up to 4 external switches |
| Rotary / footswitch / separate buttons | **6 / 4 / 10** |
| Dimensions / weight / size | **246×134×63 mm**, H65 with feet; **1.2 kg** excluding adapter; medium |
| Screen size / color / touchscreen | **? / ? / ?**; original display review still pending |
| Used IDR / new IDR | **? / ?**; no verified price observation retained for this model |
| Architecture | **4 Tone parts + 1 Rhythm part**; 1200+ tones, 70+ drum kits |
| Effect categories / choices | Each part has MFX with **93 types** and EQ; scene has **2 MFX**, each 93 types, plus reverb **7** and chorus/delay **9** types |
| Presets / memory | **150 editable Scenes, 100 factory-filled**; separate import-tone memory |
| Synth / acoustic | ZEN-Core instrument synth with instrument-style tones; exact acoustic-simulation distinction remains to be verified |
| IR / NAM / proprietary content | Roland Cloud expansions are proprietary tone content; user IR and NAM loading not established |
| Connections | **Serial GK IN/OUT**; 2×¼″ audio outputs, L shared with phones; 2×CTL/EXP; DIN MIDI IN/OUT; USB-B computer; USB-memory port; DC |
| Power / USB power | Supplied **PSB-1U adapter; 840 mA**; battery/USB bus power not listed |
| Configuration / USB functions | BOSS Tone Studio **Windows/macOS**; USB audio/MIDI, guitar-to-MIDI and re-synthesis |
| Main limitation | GK-5/GK-5B or compatible conversion required for guitar input; Serial GK is not an ordinary analog guitar jack |

The Serial GK connection uses the manufacturer's **BGK cable** and an appropriate divided pickup/interface. Older 13-pin GK equipment requires the appropriate **GKC-AD** conversion; a standard guitar cable is not a substitute. MIDI keyboards/controllers can also drive the sound engine. Primary source: [BOSS GM-800](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gm-800/).

<!-- BOSS-GT-1 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-018 --> model; this finding adds no inventory count.

### Display

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gt-1/) identifies a **132×32-dot backlit graphic LCD**. The [official front photograph](https://static.roland.com/assets/images/products/main/gt-1_main.jpg) successfully rendered and shows a **monochrome readout**; blue footswitch trim does not make it a color screen. **Diagonal is not published in the inspected source**. Explicit touchscreen hardware specification remains unverified/no touch interface documented; it is not inferred from the button layout.

The same primary specification rechecks **3 knobs,3 footswitches,12 buttons**,99 user+99 factory presets,108 effect types and32-second looper. It lists fourAA batteries or a separately sold PSA adapter, not USB bus power. These scoped checks preserve the earlier record rather than re-certifying every algorithm.

Owner manual p2 recommends keeping batteries installed for continued performance if the adapter disconnects; BATTERY LOW warning is provided. DC voltage/polarity and USB-powered operation remain unverified. [GT1-P], [GT1-M] |
| Editor / USB / platforms | Tone Studio Windows/macOS; dedicated USB driver required for computer audio. Owner manual p8 confirms patch and system-setting backup/restore, patch naming and ordering; USB2.0 cable specified. USB diagram sends effected audio to computer and mixes computer return after the chain. **USB MIX LEVEL and EFFECT OUT LEVEL each0–200%**; direct monitor is unsaved and resets ON at power-up. With direct monitor OFF, only USB return is heard unless the computer monitors the input. USB reamp is not established by that diagram. **macOS Tone Studio1.0.2 (March2018)** requires the matching USB driver. Its fixes cover incorrect parameter transfers, ASSIGN TARGET MIN/MAX input/display and unavailable delay parameters shown editable. Version1.0.1 added Sierra10.12 support; BOSS’s matrix updated2026-09-16 lists GT-1 **Hardware & Driver and Exclusive Application: Yes(Download) for macOS15 Sequoia and26 Tahoe; Under Testing for27 Golden Gate**, checked2026-10-17. Driver listings for newer macOS/Windows do not independently prove editor compatibility. Mobile editor, channels and bit depth unresolved. [GT1-M], [GT1-G], [GT1-EDITOR], [GT1-MAC] |
| Looper / routing / live use | **32 s mono**, overdub; recordings lost when looper or power is switched off. Looper overrides stored CTL1 assignments. Effect order editable; detailed routing, drums/scenes unestablished. **System1.09 (Dec2021) changes version management only, with specifications unchanged. 1.08 fixes tuner pedal combinations, held-pedal FX TYPE list behavior and Tone Studio CLEAR for CTL/ASSIGN. 1.07 fixes delayed pitch-shifted sound after patch changes.** Installed version unknown. [GT1-M], [GT1-G], [GT1-FW] |

GT-1 parameter follow-up checked2026-10-17: own Parameter Guide/Sound List ©2016, p6 type enumeration and p25 USB settings. Counts describe selector choices, not a newly reconciled108-type total. The PDF was read through the web reader; no file downloaded or visual inspection claimed.

### GT-1 — selector-count qualifications

Checked2026-10-17 against [own parameter guide][GT1-G], pp11–15. Delay choices: Standard,Pan,Reverse,Analog,Tape,Modulate,TeraEcho. Reverb slot: Ambience,Room,Hall1,Hall2,Plate,Spring,Modulate,**Delay**. Pedal: CryWah,VoWah,FatWah,LightWah,7StrWah,ResoWah,PedalBend. PedalBend is single-note,±24semitones; chords unsupported. Main delay reaches2000ms,FX SubDelay1000ms,reverb-slot Delay650ms. A following mono effect/COSM amp collapses stereo delay. Counts represent alternatives, not extra concurrent blocks; nested compressor/limiter/etc. types still need separate accounting.

### GT-1 — nested selector evidence

Checked2026-10-17, [own guide][GT1-G] pp6–10: Compressor8 (BOSS,Hi-Band,Light,D-Comp,Orange,Fat,Mild,Stereo); Limiter3 (BOSS,Rack160D,VtgRackU); ToneModify8 includes Resonator1/2/3 separately; GuitarSimulator8 pickup/body transformations; AcousticProcessor4 (Small,Medium,Bright,Power); Phaser4 (4/8/12Stage,BiPhase). T.Wah has LPF/BPF modes and Up/Down response; Chorus has Mono/Stereo1/Stereo2; SubDelay has Mono/Pan. Mode switches, continuous parameters and nested types must not be indiscriminately summed with top-level effects. Harmonist requires single-note playing; this restriction is not generalized to every pitch effect.

GT-1 owner-manual follow-up2026-10-17: own ©2016 manual pp2/8 read through web text. Battery fallback and backup scope are explicit; no USB bus-power statement was located. Absence of that statement is not proof USB power is impossible. No software, manual file or firmware downloaded; no device configuration changed.

### GT-001 — input selection and practice functions, 2026-10-17

[Official parameter guide](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-001_r_e01W.pdf), 2014, printed pp. 33/39: targeted search-index text recovered despite full-reader size limit. No visual inspection claimed.

| Field | Verified behavior / limit |
|---|---|
| I/O / input routing | INPUT SELECT chooses **GUITAR or MIC; both cannot operate simultaneously**. Each selected input passes through the effects. Dual effect chains do not establish independent simultaneous guitar/vocal processing. Adjusting MIC LEVEL or PHANTOM can interrupt sound. |
| Looper / drums / practice | **Metronome 40–250 BPM**, selectable meters and level; output through OUTPUT/PHONES. Its tempo changes MASTER BPM. This is a metronome, not evidence of a drum-pattern library. Looper remains unverified. |
| Tuner / live | Reference 435–445 Hz; MUTE silences output, BYPASS removes effects, THRU retains the current effect sound while tuning. These mode names do not establish analog true-bypass circuitry. |

### GT-001 — USB paths and specified supply, 2026-10-17

| Field | Verified behavior / limit |
|---|---|
| USB audio / routing | [Official parameter guide, p. 34](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-001_r_e01W.pdf), indexed original text: PRIMARY sends effects; its computer return mixes at the final stage. SECONDARY sends dry input; its return enters the effect-chain beginning for reamping. Both outputs are available together. Four recording channels are corroborated below; exact playback count remains unverified. |
| USB / monitoring | PRIMARY LOOP BACK sends computer playback back with processed sound. DIRECT MONITOR resets ON at startup and cannot be saved. Loopback is an audio-routing function, not a phrase looper. |
| Power / adapter identity | [GT-001-specific BOSS support notice](https://www.boss.info/jp/support/by_product/gt-001/support_documents/36a0d74b-856b-417a-862c-ae72c8f9e0bf/) specifies **PSA-100**. Its [current manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/jp/products/psa-100/) gives **9 V DC, 500 mA maximum output**, 100–240 V AC, 50–60 Hz input. Adapter capacity differs from GT-001’s 240 mA draw. Polarity remains unverified; do not assume an old adapter shares the current mains range. |

### GT-001 — own-product category counts, 2026-10-17

| Field | Verified count / restriction |
|---|---|
| Processing / preamp and drive | Counting the [model-specific published lists][GT001-P] gives **28 PREAMP choices** and **23 OD/DS choices**, including each list’s Custom entry. These are alternatives, not 28 simultaneous amps or 23 drive blocks. |
| Processing / FX1-FX2 choices | **35 distinct top-level choices across both slots**; Overtone and Tera Echo are FX2-only, so **FX1 has 33 / FX2 has 35**. This is a union count, not 35 choices in each slot or 35 simultaneous effects. Nested choices within algorithms remain uncounted. |
| Processing / nested drive restriction | A-DIST is available in the main OD/DS block and the Sub OD/DS algorithm of FX2; do not assign it to FX1’s Sub OD/DS. Aggregate simultaneous ceiling and remaining category counts still unverified. |

### GT-001 — MIDI-document category evidence, 2026-10-17

[Official MIDI implementation](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-100_GT-001_MIDI_Imple_e01_W.pdf), April 30, 2014: cover explicitly identifies GT-001 v1.00 and GT-100 v2.00. Shared entries apply only where model exclusions do not intervene. Text inspection, no execution/download.

| Field | Verified count / limitation |
|---|---|
| Processing / reverb | P27 enumerates **seven**: Ambience, Room, Hall1, Hall2, Plate, Spring, Modurate (source spelling). Time 0.1–10 s; pre-delay 0–500 ms. Choices, not seven blocks. |
| Processing / chorus | P27 gives **three modes**: Mono, Stereo1, Stereo2; not three independent chorus slots. |
| Processing / delay conflict | P27 names **ten delay types**, but encodes TYPE as 00–0A (11 values); the eleventh is unnamed. Preserve discrepancy. Main time 1–2000 ms; dual sections 1–1000 ms; Sub Delay separately 1–1000 ms. |
| Processing / nested compressor | P19/23 enumerate **eight Advanced Comp subtypes** inside FX1/FX2; not eight extra processing slots or evidence that main COMP has eight types. |
| Looper / I/O exclusions | Shared document explicitly marks phrase-loop, send/return and amp-control entries **GT-100 only**. Those controls do not establish corresponding GT-001 capabilities. |

### GT-001 — recording channels and selective reset, 2026-10-17

| Field | Verified behavior / qualification |
|---|---|
| USB audio / recording count | [Original model brochure, p. 2](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/gt-001_brochure.pdf) explicitly describes **four recording channels, two dedicated to dry audio**. Together with the PRIMARY/SECONDARY routing evidence above, this resolves DAW capture capacity; it does not independently specify the number of playback channels or permit simultaneous guitar/mic input. |
| Factory / memory reset | [Official owner’s manual, indexed p. 12](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-001_OM.pdf) allows reset selection spanning system parameters, User Quick Settings and user patches U001–U200. WRITE prepares the reset and ENTER executes it; EXIT cancels beforehand. Selected saved settings are overwritten; back up needed data through the dedicated software. This does not prove every initial user slot contains a unique factory sound. |

| Expression / wah control | Set the external expression pedal function to **WAH or WAH/FV** for real-time wah control through CTL/EXP. Pedal position and heel/toe endpoint parameters each span 0–100. A toe-position parameter does not establish a physical toe switch. |

### GT-1B — live operation and manual discrepancy

Checked 2026-10-18; original owner manual, printed pages 2, 3, 9, 11 and 12.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Looper / persistence and controls | **32 seconds mono**, with overdubbing. Disabling the looper or switching power off erases the phrase. Patch CTL1 assignments are ignored while looping. CTL1 double press stops; holding it for two seconds while stopped clears. | [GT1B-M] |
| Expression / calibration | MENU → PDL CALIBRATION: heel then ENTER, toe then ENTER; strongly press toe to test the switch, adjust THRESHOLD with knob3 if needed, then ENTER to complete. | [GT1B-M] |
| Power / battery backup | Manual recommends leaving batteries installed during adapter operation so performance can continue if the adapter disconnects. | [GT1B-M] |
| I/O / headphone load conflict | Manual printed p12 text states recommended PHONES load **44 kΩ or greater**; current official specifications state **44 Ω or greater**. Both list output impedance 44 Ω. Retain the source discrepancy; no measurement or visual symbol check was performed. | [GT1B-M]; [GT1B-S] |

AC Guitar is an acoustic-electric preamp, distinct from SFX acoustic simulation. Speaker simulation activates with global Line (Headphones); AC Guitar flat-amp simulation instead switches off in that setting. | [Manual p44][GT3-M] |
| Processing / preamp control exceptions | Match Drive ignores Middle. Match/Voxy Presence works as a high-frequency cut, displayed 0…−100. Depending on type, setting Bass/Middle/Treble all to zero may silence output. These are model-specific control behaviors. | [Manual p44][GT3-M] |
| Processing / programmable effect order | Manual explicitly permits changing effect connection order through Master → Effect Chain; insert the chosen processor at the cursor. Noise Suppressor is selected through Master and Foot Volume through Pedal/Assign. Save the resulting order using Write. This proves programmable order, not duplicated instances or a numerical simultaneous ceiling. | [Manual section 2, connection-order procedure][GT3-M] |
| Categories / delay | **Single or Tap**; Tap divides delay between stereo outputs. Delay time **0–1800 ms**, fine adjustment **0–20 ms**, with per-patch Master BPM synchronization. Tap Time **0–100%** sets right-channel time relative to left at 100%. Feedback 0–100; high cut 700 Hz–11 kHz or Flat; effect level 0–120. | [Manual pp54–55][GT3-M] |
| Processing / intelligent ring restriction | Intelligent Ring Modulator follows detected input pitch and requires single-note playing. Normal mode instead uses the internal oscillator. | [Manual p54][GT3-M] |
| Live / control assignments | **Eight assignments per patch** map controllers to parameters with target minimum/maximum, source mode and active range. **Four user assignment settings** can be reused across patches; these are not four extra sound patches. Save changes with Write. | [Manual p26][GT3-M] |
| Expression / virtual pedal triggers | Internal and Wave Pedal are virtual controllers. Internal Pedal can trigger on patch selection, built-in expression low/mid/high positions, CTL, external expression or SUB CTL1/2. Position thresholds do not establish a physical toe switch. Momentary footswitches support hold-dependent control; latch switches cannot provide that behavior. | [Manual section 2, Internal Pedal / active range][GT3-M] |
| Memory / reusable effect settings | Quick Setting stores **up to5 user settings for each effect-select button** for reuse acrosspatches. Protected preset effectsettings can seed editedpatches oruser effectsettings. This is separate from140user/200preset soundpatches andfour reusable assignmentsettings;do not add these together aspatch capacity. ChangesstillrequireWrite. | [Manual section2,p21 onward][GT3-M] |
| Preset memory | **140 user + 200 preset = 340**; initial user contents pending. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| Synth / acoustic | Guitar synth and acoustic simulation documented; Auto Riff triggers riffs from a note. Acoustic-electric preamp and anti-feedback also documented. | [Manual p5][GT3-M]; [Product][GT3-P] |
| I/O | Input, L/Mono and R outputs, stereo mini headphones, send/return, shared SUB EXP/SUB CTL1,2, MIDI In/Out; adapter input. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| Conversion | **24-bit AF A/D,64×oversampling;20-bit D/A,128×oversampling;44.1kHz**. AF combines differently scaled converter streams;not proof of24-bit D/A orUSB recording. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| I/O / analog levels | INPUT/RETURN nominal**−10dBm**,impedance**1MΩ/220kΩ** respectively. OUTPUT nominal**0dBm**,SEND**−10dBm**;bothoutput impedances**2kΩ**. Source defines0dBm=0.775Vrms;retainits terminology. These arenominal,notmaximumclipping levels. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| Conversion / dynamic range | Manufacturer specifies **≥100dB(IHF-A)** dynamicrange. Weighted publishedrating,notlocalmeasurement orunweightedSNR. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| Power / battery / USB | **14 V AC; 800 mA draw**, supplied BRC-120/230/240 adapter variants. AC has no fixed DC center polarity; battery/USB operation unverified. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| Dimensions / mass | **487 W × 222 D × 97 H mm; 4.0 kg**; adapter inclusion in mass not specified in this passage. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| IR / NAM / capture | Import, native/converted NAM and proprietary capture pending. | Unresolved |
| Editor / platforms | Editor and current OS support pending. | Unresolved |
| Live / practice | Virtual internal pedal controls parameter movement; tuner documented. Looper/drums/scenes and detailed routing pending. | [Manual p5][GT3-M] |

Rapid playing after a dual-delay patch change can miss the intended initial effect; downstream mono FX/COSM cancels stereo delay. | [Manual pp98,119–120][G10-R] |
| USB recording / MIDI routing | USB connection disables MIDI transfer through DIN connectors. USB In selects computer playback for processing/re-recording; disable software thru to prevent recirculation. USB/DGT Out Lev jointly controls USB and DIGITAL OUT level; USB Mix Level mixes computer audio with Guitar1–3 input selection. | [Manual pp90,92,95][G10-R] |
| Factory / user memory | 400 total:200 user+200 preset. Initial user-bank fill and write restrictions pending. | [Specifications][G10-S] |
| Synth / acoustic | **WAVE SYNTH and GUITAR SYNTH yes**; GUITAR SYNTH requires single notes with other strings muted and clear attacks. **GUITAR SIM acoustic simulation yes**, including single-coil/humbucker/piezo-to-acoustic choices. AC.PROCESSOR separately reshapes electro-acoustic pickup sound; Small/Medium/Bright/Power types. This supersedes unverified synth/acoustic status. | [Manual pp106–107,112][G10-R]; [Indexed official p106][G10-M]; [Acoustic-use Q&A][G10-A] |
| I/O | Quarter-inch INPUT, L/MONO/R OUTPUT, stereo PHONES, loop SEND/RETURN, AMP CONTROL; TRS EXP2/CTL3,4; USB; coaxial DIGITAL OUT; MIDI IN/OUT; DC IN. USB-B established by Roland A–B cable requirement. | [Specifications][G10-S]; [Windows support][G10-T] |
| Analog conversion / levels | 24-bit+AF A/D,24-bit D/A,44.1kHz. Input/return−10dBu; output−10/+4dBu,send−10dBu. Input1MΩ,return220kΩ; output2kΩ,send3kΩ. Dynamic range≥100dB IHF-A. | [Specifications][G10-S] |
| Power / battery / USB | 9V DC,800mA; PSB-1U supplied. Polarity, battery and USB bus operation unverified. | [Specifications][G10-S] |
| IR / NAM / capture | User IR,native/converted NAM and capture loading unverified. COSM factory modeling is not import proof. | Unresolved |
| USB / editor / platforms | USB audio/MIDI documented. Windows/Mac Librarian1.00 listed for backup/organization, not full patch-editor proof. Windows10 driver1.0.0 and macOS10.13/10.14 driver1.0.3 listed; no current-OS blanket claim. System Program1.02 is an updater, not an editor. | [Support listing][G10-F] |
| Firmware / live restrictions | System Program **1.02** fixes patch-change sound loss/abnormal noise, GRAPHIC EQ acting despite being off, and recalled effect states becoming inconsistent during rapid patch changes when Smooth mode or PHRASE LOOP is active. Also fixes FX CHAIN meter cursor tracking; improves chorus-mode transitions and category-keyword refresh. These are version-specific fixes, not proof of universal gapless switching. | [Japanese release notes][G10-U] |
| Firmware / memory maintenance | Update preserves user memory; BOSS recommends Librarian or bulk-dump backup. Transfer uses MIDI or USB with the dedicated driver and a SysEx-capable SMF utility; UpdSMF is an update transport, not a patch editor. | [Japanese release notes][G10-U] |
| Editor / Windows restrictions | Librarian1.00 manages user-patch collections; official page lists Windows XP/Vista/7/8/8.1/10. Its reference to an Editor does not identify or verify a separate full editor. Driver1.0.0 supports Windows10 32/64-bit, excludes Mobile, virtual Windows and Windows on Mac; USB3 ports must support USB2. Roland support updated2025-05-19 explicitly says **Windows11 unsupported**. This concerns the GT-10 driver, not proof every standard-audio mode fails. | [Librarian][G10-L]; [Driver][G10-D]; [Windows support][G10-T] |
| USB audio / recording modes | Standard mode uses the OS driver but **cannot use USB MIDI**; MIDI requires Advanced mode and the dedicated driver. Changing driver mode requires restart. Official support specifies a USB A–B cable, establishing USB-B on the unit. Direct Monitor controls GT-10 sound at OUTPUT/PHONES, resets On at power-up and can be controlled by ASIO2 software with the special driver. Off requires computer monitoring to hear the guitar. | [Indexed manual pp91,93][G10-M]; [Windows support][G10-T] |
| External control / assignments | Eight ASSIGN1–8 mappings. EXP1,EXP2,EXPSW,CTL1–4 can independently follow patch or system settings; system overrides ignore rather than erase patch settings. Switch behavior supports toggle/momentary. MIDI CC, input level, internal timed pedal and tempo-synced wave pedal are control sources. Foot volume is movable in-chain, distinct from post-effects overall volume. | [Workshop pp3–5,9–12][G10-W] |
| Looper / drums / live | Manufacturer Q&A confirms **38s mono /19s stereo**, overdubbing and **no saving the recorded performance**. This supports the workshop38s figure; Finnish product40s remains a conflicting regional claim. BANK pedals operate basic looping; additional assignments extend control. Manual p60 allows retaining bank switching by assigning loop control to CTL1–4; Clear Only erases immediately, while Mute/Clear first mutes and a second press erases. Drums/scenes/trails unverified. | [Q&A][G10-Q]; [Workshop pp2,5][G10-W]; [Regional product][G10-F]; [Indexed manual p60][G10-M] |

### GT-10B loop routing and bass algorithms

Checked 2026-10-18 against the manufacturer-authored 2008 manual, revision G6027127R0, as mirrored document text. No generated mirror FAQ, file download or device test used.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| I/O / external loop modes | Normal inserts the external device in series. Direct Mix blends return with the signal entering SEND/RETURN. Branch Out sends that signal out and disregards RETURN; its placement determines what reaches SEND. Send/return levels each use 0–200 display units, not documented dB. | [Own manual p131][G10B-T] |
| Synth / bass-simulator choices | BASS SIM has seven types: passive→active, active→passive, single-coil→humbucker, humbucker→single-coil, solid→hollow, single-coil→acoustic bass and humbucker→acoustic bass. Body 0–100 applies to the hollow/acoustic conversions; lower values approach piezo-like tone. These are choices within one algorithm. | [Own manual p109][G10B-T] |
| Synth / waveforms | BASS SYNTH offers Saw and Square waveforms, separate synth/direct levels and envelope-filter controls. Both waveforms belong to the same single-note algorithm; they do not establish two simultaneous synth engines. | [Own manual p110][G10B-T] |


| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Power / approved supply polarity | Named PSB-1U is specified by Roland as **9V, center-negative/sleeve-positive, 2000mA capacity**. This establishes compatible polarity; GT-10B device draw remains800mA. Regional mains/plug label must be checked separately. | [Own product][G10B-P]; [Roland supply specifications][G10B-PS] |
| I/O / connector sizes | INPUT, main OUTPUT, SEND/RETURN and AMP CONTROL are quarter-inch; PHONES stereo quarter-inch; EXP2/CTL3,4 quarter-inch TRS; SUB outputs XLR. USB connector type remains unresolved. | [Own product][G10B-P] |
| I/O / electrical levels | Nominal INPUT/RETURN−10dBu; input impedances1MΩ/220kΩ. Main OUTPUT−10/+4dBu,2kΩ; SEND−10dBu,3kΩ; SUB output600Ω. Nominal levels do not establish maximum headroom. | [Own product][G10B-P] |


| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Editor / Windows driver scope | **Driver1.0.0: Windows10 32/64-bit**, excluding Mobile. Page describes automatic internet driver delivery on connection; current delivery was not tested. Virtual Windows and Windows on Mac are unsupported. USB3 requires USB2 compatibility; try USB2 if malfunctioning. This driver page does not establish full-editor or Windows11 support. | [Official Windows driver page][G10B-W10] |
| Editor / macOS driver scope | **Driver1.0.3: macOS10.13/10.14** on Mac with onboard USB. Existing10.13 installation can be used on10.14; installer may still display10.13 wording. This does not establish newer macOS/Apple Silicon compatibility or librarian/editor support. No package downloaded or installed. | [Official Mac driver page][G10B-MAC] |

### GT-10B — channel modes and crossover

Checked2026-10-18 in the manufacturer-authored2008 manual G6027127R0, printedp133, via [manual body][G10B-T]; no generated FAQ or download.

| Field | Verified behavior / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / channel modes7 | **Single, Dual Mix, Dual L/R, Freq Divide, Dynamic SW, Dynamic A, Dynamic B**. Single uses selected A/B; Dual Mix combines both; Dual L/R sends A left andB right. These are routing choices,not seven effects or a simultaneous-block count. |
| Processing / frequency split | Freq Divide assigns lows toA andhighs toB; crossover **100Hz–4.00kHz**. This enables frequency-separated processing,not an additional synth algorithm. |
| Processing / dynamic routing | Dynamic SW selectsB with stronger picking. Dynamic A/B changes the named channel’s level with input dynamics while the other remains fixed. Sensitivity/release are0–100 display units;these do not specify attack/release milliseconds. |

### GT-10B — split outputs and conditional patch trails, 2026-10-18

[Manufacturer-authored2008 manual G6027127R0](https://manualzz.com/doc/51314063/boss-audio-systems-music-pedal-gt-10b-owner-s-manual), printedpp68–72; manual body only,not generated mirror FAQ.

| Field | Documented behavior / qualification |
|---|---|
| I/O / XLR wet-direct split | SUB OUTPUT mode L/R sends stereo normally. **Effect/Direct sends the processed stereo mix as mono to XLR L,and pre-effect mono to XLR R**. INPUT SELECT,INPUT LEVEL andPRES. still affect the direct path;it is not necessarily untouched pickup signal. DIGITAL OUT andUSB send the same digital audio source (p68),not proof of the USB bit depth. |
| Looper / live patch-change trails | **Fast discards the previous delay/reverb decay; Smooth can retain it**. Conditions include matching effect-chain placement,identical delay type/time,and compatible other-effect states/parameters. The manual warns tails may still fail in some cases. Smooth switching may be delayed **one tempo beat**;this is not unconditional gapless switching or a fixed millisecond latency. |

|---|---|

| K/F/B | Eight parameter knobs advertised; total rotary count including output level,independent footswitch/button counts require panel verification. |
| Display | Two backlit132×64 LCDs; diagonal,color/monochrome and touch remain unverified. |
| Dimensions / weight | 542×271×80mm; maximum height102mm;4.8kg. |
| Simultaneous processing / categories | Dual A/B paths; numerical simultaneous-block ceiling unverified. Product lists COMP,PREAMP,OD/DS,EQ,FX1/2,DELAY,CHORUS,REVERB,PEDAL FX,NS1/2,ACCEL FX. Current per-category totals need parameter-guide reconciliation. |
| Factory / user / total memory | 200 preset+200 user=400 locations. |
| Synth / acoustic | WAVE SYNTH,AC.GUITAR SIM and AC.PROCESSOR listed; simulation and acoustic processing are separate functions. |
| IR / native NAM / converted NAM / captures | Import/capture support unverified; do not infer from COSM modeling or USB. |
| Audio / control I/O | Quarter-inch input,L/mono+R outputs,stereo quarter-inch phones,send/return,amp control,TRS external control;stereo mini AUX;USB,DIN MIDI in/out,DC input. |

Technical table source: [BOSS product specifications and version-2 notes][GT100-P]. **Firmware limits:** A-DIST is in OD/DS and FX2 SUB OD/DS; OVERTONE and TERA ECHO are FX2-only. Do not treat the FX1/FX2 combined list as two identical freely interchangeable libraries.

### Display and scoped technical checks

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gt-1000/) establishes **one backlit graphic LCD,512×160 pixels**. It does not provide a diagonal or explicit color-depth/touch specification in the inspected text. ### GT-1000CORE — system expression-volume assignment, 2026-10-19

[Manufacturer-authored owner manual mirror](https://www.notice-facile.com/en/manual/1420090/boss%2Bgt-1000core), exact GT-1000CORE original body pp2,4,13: **PREF=SYSTEM shares expression functions across all patches**. [Own parameter guide mirror](https://manualzz.com/doc/90259078/boss-gt-1000core-guitar-effects-processor-owner-manual), original CONTROL FUNCTION table, offers EXP1/EXP2 FUNCTION=FOOT VOL. Thus system-wide target assignment is documented, not merely calibration/position recall. No device test; competing patch ASSIGN interactions remain unchecked.

[Official parameter guide eng03](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-1000CORE_parameter_eng03_W.pdf), indexed table: volume minimum/maximum0–100; curves Slow1/Slow2/Normal/Fast. Their global scope and uniform FV placement across factory presets remain unverified. Bypass, tails and looper coverage need signal-path review. Headphones share L/PHONES: leave R/MONO empty; only OUTPUT signal, not SUB OUT, reaches phones. Onboard down/up selects patches. Existing173×135mm chassis fits preference. [Capability comparison](EXPRESSION-VOLUME.md).

Official owner/parameter PDF reopening returned403; mirrors' original body and indexed official tables used, not generated diagrams or FAQs. No downloads or device operation.

### GT-1000CORE — current revision and transition qualifications

Checked2026-10-19. [Own official support](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/gt-1000core/) lists system2.02/ToneStudio4.1.1. [Exact model release history](https://www.boss.info/nl/support/by_product/gt-1000core/updates_drivers/82f27f97-d627-4536-b49c-94b317a2e0ad/) dates2.02 **July2026**: adds PLAY OPTION disabling simultaneous-switch functions; fixes conditional noise during uninterrupted patch changes and built-in-switch MANUAL behavior. **2.01,March2026 removes polyphonic tuner** and fixes seamless-switch condition checking. **1.10,January2022 fixes external-pedal ASSIGN values taking effect at startup**; installed firmware therefore matters to recall assumptions.

These changes do not certify identical FV position,headphone/bypass/tail/looper attenuation or competing patch ASSIGN behavior. Existing SYSTEM FOOT VOL evidence remains qualified. Official eng03 parameter PDF again returned no readable body; exact full current parameter-guide revision mapping remains unresolved. No firmware download/update orhardware test.

### GT-1000CORE — held-pedal recall and patch-parameter distinction, 2026-10-28

[Official eng03 parameter guide](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-1000CORE_parameter_eng03_W.pdf), indexed EXP HOLD p54; corroborating [manufacturer-authored eng03 mirror](https://www.scribd.com/document/838781699/GT-1000CORE-parameter-eng03-W), original body pp3,33,54. Official full-file access still returns403; mirror branding/title, pagination and indexed table match. Generated summaries are not evidence.

| Field | Evidence / revision qualification |
|---|---|
| Expression / position recall | **EXP HOLD ON carries pedal state across patches when the function matches**, including FOOT VOLUME→FOOT VOLUME; OFF does not. This is separate from PREF=SYSTEM target sharing and does not establish range or final-output override. |

[Official joint MIDI implementation08](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-1000_MIDI_Implementation08.pdf) explicitly names GT-1000CORE **v1.00, October1,2020**. Its applicability legend excludes starred GT-1000-only entries; the following shared entries are unstarred.

| Field | Evidence / revision qualification |
|---|---|
| Expression / parameter storage | User/temporary patch → PatchEfct contains FV minimum, maximum, position and curve. This documents patch storage separately from system-function preference; a SYSTEM range override remains unverified. This older map does not certify current2.02 behavior. |

Effect-chain placement is editable, including parallel arrangements; no single factory FV location is certified. Bypass/headphone/tail/recorded-loop attenuation and competing ASSIGNs remain unresolved; no hardware or MIDI operation was performed.

### GT-1000CORE — foot-volume and noise-detector interaction,2026-10-28

[Original BOSS parameter guide hosted by Kraft Music](https://files.kraftmusic.com/media/ownersmanual/Boss_GT-1000CORE_Parameter_Guide.pdf),57pages,©2020Roland,printedp8:Noise Suppressor **DETECT=FV OUT** follows the level after Foot Volume;BOSS recommends this when using FV instead of the guitar volume control. For protecting downstream delay sound from suppression,it instead recommends **NS INPUT**. This is a patch-routing/detector interaction,not a global volume-range override orfinal-output attenuation guarantee. Printedp27 reconfirms FV position/min/max0–100.

Exact eng03 equivalence/current2.02 coverage ofthis mirror remains unverified;the official eng03 endpoint still returns403. SYSTEM target-sharing andpatch-stored range/placement qualifications remain intact. No new exact Shopee offer surfaced;old asks are not relabelled current. No hardware test ordownload.

### GT-1000CORE — tuner bypass versus through sound

Checked2026-10-28 in [original BOSS parameter guide mirror](https://files.kraftmusic.com/media/ownersmanual/Boss_GT-1000CORE_Parameter_Guide.pdf),57pages,©2020Roland,printedp55. Tuner **MUTE** suppresses output; **BYPASS** passes the incoming guitar unchanged with all effects off; **THRU** retains the current effect sound. These are tuner modes,not three generic descriptions of normal operation. Foot-volume sweep during BYPASS versus THRU is not explicitly established; SYSTEM target sharing cannot be promoted to all-mode attenuation from these descriptions. This older guide still lists a polyphonic tuner removed in2.01,so it is not certified as current2.02 documentation. Existing firmware qualifications,patch-stored range/routing andheadphone/tail/recorded-loop gaps remain. No device test ordownload.

### GT-Pro — exact loop pairing and USB-input restriction

Checked 2026-10-19 against manufacturer-authored GT-PRO manual reproduced by ManualsLib, printed [p52](https://www.manualslib.com/manual/499099/Boss-Gt-Pro.html?page=52) and [p53](https://www.manualslib.com/manual/499099/Boss-Gt-Pro.html?page=53). Exact title,164-page structure andmatching LOOP/PRELOOP sections corroborate original manual identity;original body only,not generated Q&A.

| Field | Manual evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| I/O / stereo-loop pairing | LOOP1+2 use two send/return pairs together: **Stereo1 stereo-send/stereo-return;Stereo2 mono-send/stereo-return**. They cannot occupy different effect-chain positions. Alternatively1→2 is series and1&2 parallel. This qualifies overview wording;not three independently stereo loops. |
| I/O / loop modes | Normal inserts external processing;DirectMix blends return with loop-input dry signal;BranchOut sends outward while ignoring return. Send/return levels0–200 are relative controls,not dBu ratings. |
| I/O / PRE LOOP restriction | Fixed ahead ofINPUTLEVEL;cannot reorder. **Unavailable with InputSelect=USBIn**. Distortion placed here can impair pitch-detection effects such as guitar synth/Harmonist. This is an actual USB reprocessing restriction,not an extra USB channel. |

Earlier general loop ambiguity is superseded by these explicit manual routing details. USB stream format,control totals,memory protection andcomplete category census remain unresolved. No download orhardware operation.

### GT-Pro — pitch-shifter modes and Auto Riff memory

Fast→Medium→Slow trades slower response for reduced modulation. Pitch ±24 semitones; two-voice modes allow separate PS1/PS2 settings. Voice modes and processing modes are separate parameters, not seven additional effect families. |
| Memory / Auto Riff | **30 prepared phrases + 10 user phrases**. These are Auto Riff phrase definitions, not additional patch memories or audio-loop recordings. Phrases are triggered by picking a single note; chords are unsupported. Heavy incoming MIDI traffic may disturb playback. |
| Live / Auto Riff controls | Loop On repeats the phrase; Hold sustains the effect after input ends. Trigger sensitivity controls restarting; zero prevents retriggering. Key selection applies to prepared phrases. These controls do not establish a drum machine or persistent audio looper. |

### GT-Pro — ring modulation and vowel-mode distinctions, 2026-10-28

Intelligent follows detected input pitch and requires single notes rather than chords;Normal uses the internal oscillator without that pitch-following behavior. Frequency0–100 is relative,not a published Hz range. These are modes within sharedFX RM,not extra simultaneous blocks. |
| Processing / Humanizer | **3 modes:Picking,Auto,Random**. Picking transitions Vowel1→Vowel2 with picking;Auto cycles the selected pair;Random selects among **a,e,i,o,u**. Five vowels are choices within the effect,not five additional families. Rate/depth and mode-dependent sensitivity do not add algorithms. |
| Processing / Anti-feedback | Up to **three fixed cancellation frequencies**,each with independent depth. Frequency0–100 is a control range,not Hz or a three-family count. This suppresses guitar-body resonance;it is separate from the Feedbacker effect. |

Exact GT-PRO title andsection pagination;indexed original text,not generated FAQs. No full-page visual review,download or device test.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / 2×2 Chorus | Two stereo choruses process separate low/high frequency bands;**crossover100Hz–4.00kHz**. Internal frequency split within one FX-2 family,not two freely placed chorus blocks or two guitar inputs. |
| Synth / Acoustic Processor | Processes an acoustic-electric pickup toward a close-microphone-like sound. Distinct from Guitar Simulator electric-to-acoustic transformations;does not require or establish a physical mic input. |
| Looper / Sound Hold | Sustains a played note while later melody is played;**two or more simultaneously played notes do not work properly**. Hold Off/On,relative RiseTime0–100,EffectLevel0–120;normally controlled via external CTL/FC-200. Not a specified-duration phrase looper or evidence of audio-file storage. |
| Processing / Sub Delay | Separate FX-2-only delay with **0–400ms** delay-time range and BPM synchronization. Not the dedicated DELAY block’s1800ms Single type or2.8second Hold recording. |

Remaining:Slicer/Rotary andother outstanding FX definitions,44-category reconciliation,USBchannelcount andqualified IDR. Existingfamily andpatchcounts unchanged.

### GT-Pro — Assign Hold position recall (2026-10-28)

Manufacturer-authored original **GT-PRO manual p99**, indexed [English PDF reproduction](https://c3.zzounds.com/media/GT-PRO_e2-c15bef3b6baf301921f986b46ca7c501.pdf), corroborated by [same English page](https://www.manualshelf.com/manual/roland/gt-pro/owner-s-manual-english/page-99.html) and [Spanish manufacturer manual p99](https://www.manualslib.es/manual/12527/Boss-Gt-Pro.html?page=99). Exact model, chapter and pagination match earlier original-manual checks. Indexed original body only: direct PDF exceeds reader size limit and English HTML reopening missed cache; no visual/device test or download.

| Setting | Documented recall behavior / limitation |
|---|---|
| Assign Hold On | Carries the pedal-assignment state across patch changes. Volume-to-volume example follows the pedal’s current angle. If the destination assigns wah instead, its volume takes the patch value and wah follows the pedal angle. This is position/state retention, **not a global target lock**. |
| Assign Hold Off | Destination volume initially uses its saved patch value; subsequent pedal movement/information changes it according to the pedal. No uninterrupted held-position volume guarantee. |
| Toggle source | Assign Hold does not operate when Assign Source mode is Toggle. This restriction is distinct from a continuous analog pedal’s calibration. |

This resolves the documented ASSIGN recall examples, not SYSTEM fixed FootVolume versus dedicated FV enable/MIN/MAX precedence or EXP2 memo ambiguity. The earlier competing-source warning remains applicable. Bypass/phones/tails/recorded-Hold coverage remains qualified separately.

### GT-Pro — historical USB device names and channel-count boundary

Checked 2026-10-28 against [original manufacturer manual, indexed printed p123](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf), exact GT-PRO Windows driver-settings section. Audio input/output use **BOSS GT-PRO** under WDM, **MME BOSS GT-PRO In/Out** under MME, and **BOSS GT-PRO** as ASIO device. MIDI input/output instead use **BOSS GT-PRO Control**, for editor/sequencer SysEx communication with the unit. These driver-era endpoint names do not establish USB audio channel count or DIN-MIDI bridging.

The same section advises disabling application monitoring or using ASIO Direct Monitor to avoid feedback/double monitoring. Its MME fallback concerns applications lacking WDM/ASIO, including24-bit recording/playback; not a current Windows compatibility certification. Existing Standard/Special resolution and modern-host restrictions remain. USB channel count still requires explicit documentation; MAIN+SUB mixing is not evidence of four recording channels. No driver/software download or device test.

### GT-Pro — noise-suppressor detector and volume-tail interaction

Checked 2026-10-28 against [original manufacturer manual, indexed printed p54](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/GT-PRO_OM.pdf), exact MASTER/NS table. **Detect selects Input, NS Input or FV Out**: input-jack signal, noise-suppressor input or signal after Foot Volume respectively. **Threshold and Release each0–100**, not stated dB/milliseconds. High threshold can silence quiet guitar-volume settings. The manufacturer recommends NS before reverberation effects to avoid interrupting decay.

Thus Foot Volume can affect the detector signal when FV Out is selected; this is a documented control-path interaction, not proof of final-output placement or universal tail muting. MASTER button itself does not toggle NS; the on/off parameter uses PATCH/VALUE. Global FV assignment/range precedence and bypass/phones/recorded-Hold coverage remain qualified. The44-category/13-versus15 counting discrepancy is still unresolved; no invented category added to force agreement. No download or device test.

## GX-1

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. This is GX-1, not GX-1B. Full original facts and observation dates remain below. Historical next-model notes are provenance; current work is in research-state.json.

<!-- BOSS-GX-1 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing model in [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-022 -->; not an additional catalogue entry and not GX-1B.

### Display and directly rechecked hardware

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-1/) confirms **one color graphic LCD,320×240 pixels**. A published diagonal was not located. **Touchscreen remains unverified/no touch interface documented**; references to touching the separate effect-category buttons do not prove touchscreen hardware.

The enumerated controls confirm **3 knobs,3 main footswitches,14 separate buttons**, plus expression/toe switching. There are99 user+99 factory memories and146 effect types. Eight simultaneous effects plus volume/noise suppression are distinct from the total model library.

Power is explicitly **fourAA batteries,PSA adapter or USB bus**, with250mA DC/battery and400mA USB draw. The official [GX-1 support page](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-1/support/) confirms Windows/macOS/iOS/Android Tone Studio, built-in Bluetooth and WAV IR loading. Those checks do not complete the remaining algorithm/IR-capacity fields.

### Separate IDR observations

| Channel | Amount | Evidence and qualification |
|---|---:|---|
| Shopee V Musik Central | **Rp4.743.000** | [shop29988284/item27644034847](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-GX-1-prosesor-efek-gitar-multi-efek-%28multi-effects%29-gx1-Boss-Original-i.29988284.27644034847); indexed hardware ask, checkout unverified |
| Shopee seller named Boss Official Shop | **Rp5.410.000 — SOLD OUT** | [shop341882414/item56705406518](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-GX-1-Guitar-Effects-Processor-Multi-Efek-Pedal-Ultra-Portable-i.341882414.56705406518); Habis explicitly shown |

| Used | **Unknown** | No condition-verified Indonesian used offer established |

The lower retailer amount and sold-out Shopee ask are separate observations, not bounds of a confirmed active range. A mixed GX-1/GX-1B title with adapter variations was excluded from per-model pricing. The GX-1B bass listing is not an interchangeable GX-1 quote. Condition detail, warranty, accessories, shipping, region and seller authenticity remain unverified.

Original USD evidence stays historical and is not relabelled as IDR. Remaining display work is the published diagonal and explicit touch specification. **Next existing-model review: ME-90.**

### GX-1 — original USD and technical provenance

Research date **2026-10-14**. Technical sources are the linked manufacturer's specifications, including current firmware-dependent figures. **Partial records**: `?` means unverified. Dimensions are **W × D × H in mm**; `/max` gives the maximum height with the pedal raised. `K/F/B` = rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons, excluding the expression toe switch and rear power/mode switches. Price columns are **USD US-market listed/asking observations**, not statistically estimated fair values; see sources below.

“Large” here is a mechanical filter result, not the manufacturer's marketing classification: the GX-1 misses both the 200×140 compact limit and the 300 mm medium-width limit.

Model totals have different inclusion rules. ME-90's selectable alternatives are not all simultaneously present on its panel. A missing NAM claim is not converted into a definitive “No” without model/firmware documentation.

Price retrieval date: **2026-10-14**. Indexed older listings are explicitly less reliable than current, purchasable offers. No guarantee of stock, local Indonesian pricing, delivery cost or completed-sale value.

GX-1 HTML FX table lists38 families; OVERTONE appears separately in its additional-information list/footnote and is **FX3-only**. Thus39 documented FX families including OVERTONE,38 available to FX1/2; same-effect reuse permitted. Counts are families, not all subtypes or simultaneous blocks. Other category subtotals unresolved. [GX1-P], [GX1-FX] |
| Factory / user / total memory | **99 preset +99 user =198**. [GX1-P] |
| Synth / acoustic | **AC. GTR SIM** and **AC RESO** explicitly documented; latter processes electro-acoustic pickup sound. Sitar simulation, Humanizer and OVERTONE do not by themselves prove a general synth engine. [GX1-FX] |
| IR / NAM / capture | **8 IR slots**, WAV **44.1/48/96 kHz**, **16/24-bit linear or32-bit float**. Joint official GX-1/GX-1B editor manual explicitly covers both models. Native NAM, converted NAM and proprietary capture imports unestablished. [GX1-IR] |

| Power / battery / USB power | Four AA, PSA adapter or USB bus. Draw250 mA DC/battery,400 mA USB. USB adapter **5 V/500 mA or greater**; USB takes priority over batteries. Connecting the PSA while on transfers supply to PSA. DC voltage/polarity remain unverified. [GX1-P], [GX1-IO] |
| Editor / USB / platforms | Tone Studio Windows/macOS/iOS/Android; built-in Bluetooth. USB VENDOR uses dedicated computer driver; GENERIC uses standard iOS functions with limits. Dry output, DAW-to-effects input and loopback documented. Direct monitor resets ON at power-up. USB channels/bit depth and Android USB compatibility unresolved. [GX1-P], [GX1-USB] |
| Looper / live use | **38 s mono /19 s stereo**, overdub. UP+CTL1 toggles looper; stored CTL1 assignments ignored while active. Recording persistence, full routing/scenes/drums remain unchecked. [GX1-LOOP] |

### GX-1 — USB gain and routing qualifications

[Exact USB parameter documentation](https://static.roland.com/manuals/gx-1_parameter/en-US/368274827368317323.html),checked2026-10-19;HTML has no explicit document revision. No driver downloaded ordevice operated.

| USB / level controls and routing | EFX OUT LVL, MIX LEVEL, DRY OUT and DRY TO EFX each range0–200%. EFX OUT is processed sound tocomputer;MIX LEVEL returns computer audio at the chain final stage;DRY OUT sends unprocessed guitar;DRY TO EFX feeds computer audio into effects. These controls establish routing,not channel enumeration orstream bit depth. |
| USB / monitor and loopback distinction | LOOPBACK sends the computer/effect mix back tocomputer. DIRECT MONITOR OFF requires computer through-monitoring tohear the guitar;only USB return is otherwise heard. Direct-monitor state is unsaved andreturnsON atstartup. |

### GX-1B — desktop editor requirements

Checked 2026-10-18 against the separately listed Windows and Mac application pages.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Editor / application identity and firmware | Desktop **BOSS TONE STUDIO for GX-1 1.0.0** explicitly supports GX-1B and requires **GX-1B firmware 1.03 or later**. The application name is shared; the separate GX-1 minimum is 1.02 and must not be substituted for the bass model. Both pages describe a USB data connection. | [Windows application](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/gx-1b/updates_drivers/8beff124-53fa-4bae-b997-9032a9b31072/); [Mac application](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/gx-1b/updates_drivers/20dd9dd9-85f2-42f4-9662-56874e660049/) |
| Editor / platform and network scope | Mac application explicitly supports **Apple silicon and Intel processors**. Both application pages refer OS versions to the **Exclusive Application** compatibility table, separately from Hardware & Driver. Tone Exchange requires internet. These pages do not establish USB stream format or offline Tone Exchange access. | [Windows application](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/gx-1b/updates_drivers/8beff124-53fa-4bae-b997-9032a9b31072/); [Mac application](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/gx-1b/updates_drivers/20dd9dd9-85f2-42f4-9662-56874e660049/) |

### GX-1B — USB driver modes and routing

Checked 2026-10-18 against the model-specific parameter and reference manuals.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| I/O / USB driver modes | **VENDOR** is specified for Windows/macOS and requires the GX-1B driver. **GENERIC** uses standard iOS functionality, with some functions potentially limited versus VENDOR. This does not establish generic Windows/macOS support or unrestricted Android compatibility. | [USB parameters](https://static.roland.com/manuals/gx-1b_parameter/en-US/392593931399205515.html) |
| I/O / dry and processed USB routing | EFX OUT LVL controls processed output; DRY OUT sends unprocessed bass. DRY TO EFX controls computer input to the effects, while MIX LEVEL places computer return at the final chain stage. Each has a 0–200% range. This documents dry recording/reprocessing routes; exact stream channels and bit depth remain unverified. | [USB parameters](https://static.roland.com/manuals/gx-1b_parameter/en-US/392593931399205515.html) |
| I/O / monitoring and loopback | Direct monitor switches the unit sound to OUTPUT/PHONES and resets ON at startup; the setting cannot be saved. LOOPBACK sends computer audio mixed with processed bass back to the computer. | [USB parameters](https://static.roland.com/manuals/gx-1b_parameter/en-US/392593931399205515.html) |
| Editor / backup scope | Reference manual explicitly includes backup and restoration of both memories and system settings, alongside memory naming, ordering and sharing. | [Computer connection](https://static.roland.com/manuals/gx-1b_reference/en-US/385298827387664523.html) |

### GX-1B — approved adapter, macOS status and offer recheck

Checked 2026-10-18.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Power / approved DC adapter | The GX-1B manual specifies PSA-S series at DC IN. Official PSA-S specifications give **9 V regulated DC, center negative, 500 mA capacity**; this is the named adapter output, distinct from GX-1B's 250 mA DC/battery draw. Regional adapter compatibility applies. USB power is a separate 5 V supply path. | [GX1B-C]; [Official PSA-S specification](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/201953449-BOSS-PSA-S-Adaptor-Specifications) |
| Editor / dated macOS compatibility | Table updated **2026-09-16** marks GX-1B **Hardware & Driver** and **Exclusive Application** each **Yes(Download) for macOS 15/26**, **Under Testing for 27**. This is manufacturer status, not a local test or future compatibility promise. | [Official compatibility table](https://www.boss.info/global/support/support_news/25090811m1/) |
| IDR / Salomo offer recheck | Exact seller/item **1776275/52656028208**, Jakarta Utara: cached body crawled six days earlier again shows **Rp4.868.000, Dengan Voucher**, minimum Rp10.000/10% discount wording, cap unspecified. No Habis text in retrieved body; this is not stock proof. Condition, adapter bundle and warranty region remain unknown; late-delivery Rp10.000 compensation is separate from the hardware voucher. | [Exact Shopee hardware listing](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-GX1B-Pedal-Bass-Multi-Effects-Original-i.1776275.52656028208) |

## GX-10

Consolidated and refreshed2026-10-16;partial technical verification. Original dates/contradictions retained. Historical next-model notes below are provenance;current queue is in research-state.json.

<!-- BOSS-GX-10 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing model in [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-014 -->, not an additional catalogue entry.

### Display and directly rechecked specifications

The [manufacturer page](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-10/) explicitly specifies **one color graphic LCD,480×272 pixels, with touchscreen**. **Diagonal remains unknown**: a numeric size was not located in the checked primary text and is not inferred from the chassis or retailer wording. The [manufacturer getting-started article](https://articles.boss.info/getting-started-with-the-boss-gx-10/) confirms actual finger-based editing and says the display is the same design introduced on GX-100.

The current product specification confirms **6 knobs,3 footswitches,5 buttons**, plus expression/toe and power separately;15 assignable blocks;32 amps split23 guitar/9 bass;170 effects. Windows/macOS editing and **iOS/Android editing with optional BT-DUAL** remain distinct from ordinary USB mobile recording. This scoped review does not independently certify the complete effect-category inventory or every earlier power/loading field.

## GX-100

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-030 --> record, not another model.

### Display and firmware-sensitive correction

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-100/) confirms a **color graphic LCD,480×272 pixels, with touchscreen**. A numeric diagonal was not found in the checked primary source. Retailers/reviews commonly claim4.3 inches, but this pass does not promote that to manufacturer-verified diagonal evidence.

The official page now advertises **32 AIRD amps**, rather than the launch23. Its version2.0 section explains three added guitar and six added bass amps, seven new effects and eight new factory presets. Current specification still lists170 effect types and100 preset+200 user memories; overlapping marketing counts are not summed. The control list rechecks **6 knobs,8 footswitches,7 buttons**, plus expression toe/power and other rear switches separately.

Windows/macOS Tone Studio now imports speaker IRs without a separate loader application. iOS/Android wireless editing continues to require optional BT-DUAL. Full category subtotals, exact IR capacity and remaining synth/acoustic algorithms are not independently re-certified in this scoped pass.
