## IR-200 — partial expression capability lead

Checked 2026-10-19; outside active inventory pending full-profile/scope audit. [Manufacturer-authored 2021 manual, original PDF mirror](https://manuals.plus/m/b9825b3bebd56cb099901e5e15ff82bed29bc40deea57a2f0c884f79aaf4a363_optim.pdf),26pages, Roland copyright, IR-200 panel and model-specific routing identify source; official manual landing timed out. Printedp17: **EXP PDL FUNCTION PREF=SYSTEM** makes settings common across memories. Targets include AMP LEVEL, AMP GAIN, AMP SOL LV, SEND LEVEL, RET LEVEL; **not an established final-output volume target**. MIN/MAX0–100;AMP GAIN maximum120. Prefer verifying AMP LEVEL with amp bypass, ambience, parallel return andAUX before promising whole-signal mute. No such coverage certified.

Printedpp3–6 document normal guitar/bass input, dedicated phones, CTL1,2/EXP withEV-30/EV-5, and onboard previous/next memory switches. Headphone level usesMEMORY knob;its independence doesnot prove expression coverage. Footprint, full model fields, qualifying multi-effects scope and exact Shopee offer remain pending; no active admission or sibling specification transfer. No downloads or hardware test.

### IR-200 — manufacturer footprint and processing follow-up

Checked 2026-10-19. [Exact BOSS product/specifications](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ir-200/) publishes **101W×138D×63H mm**,65H including feet;660g excluding adapter. Fits preferred chassis; plugs require clearance. Eight guitar/three bass amps, noise suppressor, two memory EQs plus two global EQs, and Room/Studio/Hall ambience establish multiple processing stages; final catalogue scope/admission remains pending full profile. **128 setup memories**;two simultaneous IRs;144 BOSS+10 Celestion factory IRs. User capacity128mono/64stereo WAV;up to32bitfloat/96kHz,200/500ms. Graphic132×32 backlit LCD;diagonal/color/touch unresolved. Published335mA draw,adapter supply;voltage/polarity andbattery/USBpower require exact confirmation. Controls list9rotaries/2foot/2buttons;no visual recertification.

[Indexed Shopee search-page lead](https://shopee.co.id/search?keyword=bos+slot) mentions IR-200 atRp7.869.000,Jakarta Pusat,without exact seller/item. **Not an accepted hardware offer**: condition,stock,variant,bundle,warranty,voucher,total unknown. No new/used range established. Global AMP LEVEL coverage gaps above remain open; no downloads or hardware test.

### IR-200 — pedal hold and USB routing boundaries

Checked 2026-10-19 against the [same manufacturer-authored manual, pp13–19](https://manuals.plus/m/b9825b3bebd56cb099901e5e15ff82bed29bc40deea57a2f0c884f79aaf4a363_optim.pdf). AMP LEVEL controls the entire preamp, not documented final output. **EXP PDL HOLD** separately determines whether operational state carries across memory changes; it does not establish assignment scope.

USB MIX IN joins at the final MAIN OUT stage in Vendor and Generic modes. Therefore AMP LEVEL must not be promised as an all-source mute; exact bypass/ambience/headphone attenuation remains unverified. Vendor mode is for Windows/macOS with driver; Generic uses standard iOS functions with reduced features. Vendor offers separate dry out, effects input/output and loopback controls; these are not a verified numerical USB channel count.

Send/Return can sit before cabinets or after EQ; NORMAL inserts external sound in series, DIR MIX combines return and direct sound. Send/return audio levels0–200 are distinct from expression MIN/MAX0–100. Headphone volume and ambience each have0–100 settings. No hardware, firmware or software operation.

### IR-200 — firmware history and exact hardware offer

Checked 2026-10-19. [Official system-program history](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/ir-200/updates_drivers/fbe01ca3-1511-48ce-ab5a-72482a54dadf/) lists **1.10 (January 2025)**: AUTO OFF choices OFF/20MIN/10HRS, default 20MIN. Version **1.02 (March 2022)** extends EXP HOLD to MIDI EXP CC values and fixes parameter changes sent simultaneously with memory changes, plus CONCERT BASS output noise. These changes do not establish a global final-output volume target. Seller firmware remains unknown; nothing downloaded or installed.

[Exact Shopee hardware item](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-IR200-IR-200-Amp-IR-Cabinet-i.16879370.21523624968): **Seven Stereo Music & Audio Store**, shop16879370/item21523624968, Kota Bandung, cached ask **Rp7.777.500**. Reader reports a five-month cache and “Layanan Tidak Tersedia”; service unavailability does not establish sold-out stock. Condition/new-versus-used, working state, stock, variant/bundle, warranty, voucher, shipping/tax and checkout total remain unknown. Not a completed sale or current-stock confirmation. The older Rp7.869.000 search-page lead remains separate, not this item’s price history. Power voltage/polarity and full-profile admission remain unresolved.

### IR-200 — original manual and IR Loader qualification

Checked 2026-10-19. The [original BOSS English manual](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/IR-200_eng01_W.pdf), ©2021,26 pages, is now accessible; the earlier mirror and access history remain preserved. Printed pp3–6 confirm simultaneous footswitches bypass all effects; **AUX IN mixes to OUTPUT and PHONES**, with level adjusted at the player. This supplies another reason not to promise AMP LEVEL mutes every source. Bypass attenuation itself remains unverified. DC IN insertion/removal switches power; p23 specifies adapter supply and335mA, but no numerical DC voltage/polarity. Battery/USB operation remains unverified. Listed expression options also include FV-500H/FV-500L alongside EV-30/EV-5.

[Original IR Loader manual](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/IR-200_IR_Loader_eng01_W.pdf), ©2021,3 pages: Windows and Mac installers require the corresponding USB driver. It documents IR import, naming, deletion, favorites, and whole-device memories/settings backup/restore; this does not establish a full parameter editor or mobile editor. Accepted WAV rates44.1/48/96kHz,16/24-bit linear or32-bit float,200/500ms. **For a stereo file, it asks the user to select one channel.** Preserve this exact loader behavior beside the product page’s broader stereo-file wording and prior capacity description; true stereo storage/path handling needs clarification. No NAM/capture support inferred. Nothing downloaded, installed or tested.

### IR-200 — exact adapter association and remaining checklist

Checked 2026-10-19. [Roland/BOSS adapter correspondence, updated 2025-07-15](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/roland_ac_adaptor_information.pdf?lang=fr-FR), p3, explicitly pairs **IR-200 with PSA-230S**; p1 specifies9.0V DC/0.5A output and100–240V AC,50/60Hz input. [Roland PSA-S electrical specification](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/201953449-BOSS-PSA-S-Adaptor-Specifications), updated2026-06-16, confirms regulated9V DC, **center negative**,0.5A. This resolves the documented supply voltage/polarity through an exact model-to-adapter association, not a sibling processor. **500mA is adapter capacity;335mA remains the device draw.** Regional plug and seller-supplied adapter identity/condition remain unverified. Earlier unknowns above describe prior research stages.

### KATANA:GO — direct analog expression screen

Checked 2026-10-28 against [own current manufacturer specifications][KTGO-P]. Ordinary guitar/bass input is documented. Published connectors are the instrument plug, stereo-mini PHONES and USB-C: **no direct analog EXP socket**. EV-1-WL or an expression pedal via FS-1-WL is an indirect wireless/MIDI route, not an analog pedal connection to this processor. Global analog-volume assignment, placement/endpoints/recall are therefore inapplicable to the requested path.

Current body **45W×125Dmm** fits preference; preserve earlier physical-design and seller-GO2 qualifications. Dedicated headphones are documented; **no onboard footswitches** in the control inventory. A/B/C and multifunction buttons are hand controls. Wireless-volume bypass, headphone, Bluetooth/USB backing-audio, tail and recorded-loop coverage remain unverified. A physical master control does not establish equivalent wireless target coverage. No controller, software or hardware operation.

### ME-5 — official indexed manual recovery

Checked2026-10-17. Direct [official PDF][ME5-M] still haszero extracted lines,but targeted search returned its own original section “Function of each parameter”,printed p36. Evidence below comes from that indexed passage,not ManualsLib generated FAQs or a visual inspection. ManualsLib pages3/6 returned navigation only;page17 inaccessible.

| Field | Evidence / unresolved conflict |
|---|---|
| Processing / reverb-delay | One shared final block chooses **Room,Hall1,Hall2,Plate,Gated,orDelay**. Five reverb modes plusone delay alternative,not simultaneous reverb+delay. |
| Processing / delay-time conflict | Roland specification says **1–500ms**;official indexed p36 panel text instead shows **0.1–500ms**. Preserve lower-limit conflict pending corroboration;maximum500ms consistent. |
| I/O / routing | Indexed chain:Compressor→Overdrive/Distortion→EQ→Chorus/Flanger→send/return+noise-suppression/expression area→Reverb/Delay→Master. Expression located before final ambience block;precise assignment behavior remains pending readable text. |

### ME-5 — indexed memory and modulation evidence

Checked2026-10-17 against targeted indexed passages of [official original manual][ME5-M],printed pp5,33. Direct PDF text remains unavailable;no visual inspection.

| Field | Verified evidence / limits |
|---|---|
| Factory / user memory | **64 manufacturer-programmed,rewritable locations** arranged4groups×4banks×4numbers. Creating a sound starts from a patch that may be erased;write is required. Unsaved edits are lost on power-off or selecting another operating mode. No separate64-slot factory bank established. |
| Processing / chorus-flanger | Five alternatives in one block:mode1 chorus;modes2–5 flanger. Higher flanger-mode numbers move processing toward higher frequency bands. Not five simultaneous modulation blocks. |

The same manual's indexed outline describes five effect units and their stored on/off states;an explicit maximum simultaneous statement still needs corroboration. Overdrive-mode details remain pending;ME-6B search results are not ME-5 evidence.

### ME-5 — simultaneous units and MIDI memory addressing

Checked2026-10-17 against indexed original [official manual][ME5-M]:“Operation/ModeSelection”,“PatchEditing”,and“MIDI Implementation Version1.00”. Search excerpts only;no visualinspection.

| Field | Verified behavior / limits |
|---|---|
| Processing / simultaneous | **All five units can be on together**:Compressor,Overdrive/Distortion,EQ,Chorus/Flanger,Reverb/Delay. Shared alternatives remain shared;noise suppression/external loop are additional functions. Manual cautions high-gain combinations may generate noise/oscillation. |
| Processing / drive | MIDI parameter table defines **three modes,1–3**,encoded0–2. Individual mode descriptions remain unverified;do not substitute ME-6B labels. |
| Controls / mechanism | ModeSelect is a push button cyclingPlay→Edit→MIDI;Manual entered separately. PatchEditing describes display/parameter/value editing instead of physical parameter knobs. Supports text-derived0rotary/5foot/8buttons inventory;power separate. |
| MIDI / memory | Transmit programs1–64;receive1–128,with65–128 mapped back to1–64. Transmit/receive channel cannot differ. SysEx bulk all/current andparameter communication documented;these are preset/parameter data,not evidence of audio/IR/NAM import. |

| Processing / categories | Compressor; drive; Humanizer/EQ; Harmonist/Pitch; Chorus/Flanger/Phaser/Tremolo; delay; reverb; suppression; amp simulator. **23 effects available; up to 11 simultaneously**. Choice counts and exclusions remain partly unresolved. | [Manual pp3,34][ME8-M] |
| Categories / drive and harmonies | **Five analog drive alternatives**: Overdrive, Turbo Overdrive, Blues Driver, Distortion, Hard Distortion (Metal Zone); External selects a connected drive. Harmonist adds **two harmony notes** to the guitar, producing three-note harmony. | [Manual pp3,20][ME8-M] |
| Live / control and transitions | CTL function is assignable per patch. Manual pedals switch individual effects; tempo pedals set delay time/rate. Delay/reverb tails continue when moving to a patch without those effects. Exact transition restrictions pending. | [Manual p3][ME8-M] |
| Processing / Humanizer or EQ | **Mutually exclusive** Humanizer and three-band EQ. Humanizer alternates two vowels selected independently from u/o/i/e/a; trigger can restart from vowel 1 on a played string. EQ has parametric mids (63 Hz–11 kHz) and separate Presence. Level ranges −18…18 are displayed parameter values; dB units not established by this passage. | [Manual p22][ME8-M] |
| Processing / harmony or pitch | Harmonist and Pitch Shifter are alternatives. Harmonist requires **single-note playing**, with two harmony levels, direct level and major/minor song key. Pitch Shifter supports chords; two shifted voices each cover **−12…+12 semitones**, with independent Fine −20…20 (units unestablished here). | [Manual pp23–24][ME8-M] |
| Categories / shared modulation | **Nine alternatives**: Chorus, Stereo Chorus, Flanger, Hi Band Flanger, Phaser 1–4, Tremolo. Phaser 1/2/3 use four/eight/ten phases; Phaser 4 chains two phasers. Indexed MODE summary repeats PH2 where the detailed list identifies PH3; preserve that transcription discrepancy. | [Manual p24][ME8-M] |
| Categories / reverb | **Six alternatives**: Hall 1/2, Room 1/2, Plate 1/2. TIME **1–20 is a displayed parameter range**, with seconds not established by the passage; Tone −50…50 and effect level 0…100. | [Manual p27][ME8-M] |
| Expression / control-effect restrictions | **Slow Attack is CTL-only; Foot Volume is EXP-only**. Foot Volume preserves delay/reverb decay when lowering the pre-ambience signal. Feedbacker requires a stable single note; CTL press raises feedback and release mutes it, while EXP varies its level. Intelligent Ring Modulator recommends single notes and remains ring-modulated whenever selected. Pedal Wah supports CTL or continuous EXP. | [Manual p13][ME8-M] |
| Categories / control targets | Assignments include Pedal Wah, Feedbacker, Ring Modulator, Intelligent Ring Modulator, Slow Attack, Foot Volume, Arm Down 1–3, Arm Up 1–2, Trip 1–2, Vibrato, Silent, Tempo, OD/DS, HR/PS, modulation, Delay, Reverb and Tuner; Off additional. Arm Down 1 reaches two octaves down. These are assignable choices, not additional simultaneous blocks. | [Manual p13][ME8-M] |
| Expression / Arm, Trip and Vibrato | Arm Down 2 lowers two semitones; Down 3 one octave; Up 1 raises three semitones; Up 2 one octave. Trip 1 adds a voice up to two octaves above dry; Trip 2 adds one up to an octave below dry. CTL drives timed pitch movement; EXP follows pedal position. Vibrato: CTL gradually applies it while held; EXP varies depth and lifted position disables it. | [Manual p14][ME8-M] |
| Live / Silent, Tempo and remote restrictions | **CTL-only**: Silent mutes all outputs and clears audible delay/reverb tails; Tempo requires more than four presses; Remote toggles drive, harmony/pitch, modulation, delay, reverb or tuner. These targets do not work with EXP. Silent is distinct from Foot Volume, which preserves ambience decay. | [Manual p14][ME8-M] |
| Categories / amp simulator and suppression | Amp simulator selects **Small Box or Large Box**, with Off additional. Noise-suppression threshold spans Off–100; excessive threshold can suppress quiet playing. Master Level 80 gives equal input/output level when all effects are off. CTL and optional EXP have separate assignment parameters. | [Manual p28][ME8-M] |
| Preset memory | **50 user + 50 preset = 100**, plus Manual settings;user locations initially duplicate presets,as clarified below. | [Manual p34][ME8-M] |
| Synth / acoustic | Dedicated synthesis/acoustic simulation unverified. | Unresolved |

### ME-8 — tempo-delay ceiling and shared tempo

Checked2026-10-18 against manufacturer-indexed [own manual p16][ME8-M], How to Set the Patch for Using the Tempo Delay/Tempo Rate. No PDF downloaded or device operation performed.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Delay / tempo ceiling | Tempo Delay supports intervals **t-1–t-9**, with **2.0seconds maximum**. When basic tempo plus interval would exceed that maximum, the actual delay time is **halved**. Do not assume the selected subdivision is preserved beyond the ceiling. |
| Live / simultaneous tempo functions | Tempo Delay and Tempo Rate can operate together in a patch. Humanizer,Flanger,Phaser andTremolo offer tempo-rate settings;Flanger/Phaser also offer tempo-step settings. These parameter options are not extra effect blocks. |
| Live / basic tempo persistence | Optional **FS-5U at TEMPO IN** or CTL assigned TEMPO sets tempo after **more than four steady presses**. Basic tempo starts at **quarter-note120** at each power-on and retains changes until power-off;it can be changed even while the current patch does not use it. No patch-stored tempo or MIDI clock support is inferred. |


### ME-8 — delay choices, compressor and initialization

Checked2026-10-18 against manufacturer-indexed own-manual pp20,26 and Roland support. Documentation only; no reset, device test or source-file download.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Category choices / delay2 | **Mono and Tap** are the two delay types. TIME spans **1ms–2.00s**;Tap is intended for stereo output and its actual delay time is **half the set value**. This mode behavior is separate from the tempo-ceiling rule above. With feedback0,Mono produces one repeat;Tap produces one delayed sound on each side. [Own manual p26][ME8-M] |
| Category choices / compressor | One described compressor has Sustain/Attack/Level0–100 andTone−50–50. LowSustain behaves like limiting;this is a parameter behavior,not a separately selectable limiter model. Displayed values do not establish milliseconds orcompression-ratio units. [Own manual p20][ME8-M] |
| Memory / factory reset scope | Roland warns that initialization loses **all user information**. Its documented PARAMETER-left/down power-on andWRITE/COPY confirmation restores factory state;it is not a settings-only reset orproof of an external backup editor. [Manufacturer support](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/201957579-ME-8-Initialization-Factory-Reset) |


### ME-8 — conversion, analog levels and Manual memory

Checked2026-10-18. [Roland’s model-specific specification](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/201925899-ME-8-Technical-Specifcations),updated2026-10-02, and manufacturer-indexed own-manual p17. No hardware measurements or device operation.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Conversion / sample rate | **18-bit Delta-Sigma ADC;DAC accepts20-bit input with4×oversampling digital filter/Delta-Sigma conversion;44.1kHz**. This describes internal conversion,not USB audio or a claim of20-bit ADC resolution. [Roland specification](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/201925899-ME-8-Technical-Specifcations) |
| I/O / analog electrical specifications | Input impedance **1MΩ**,output impedance **5.7kΩ**;nominal input/output **−20dBm**,with source defining0dBm=0.775Vrms. Retain the manufacturer’s unit convention;these are nominal levels,not maximum headroom orproof of balanced output. [Roland specification](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/201925899-ME-8-Technical-Specifcations) |
| Memory / Manual settings persistence | Manual-mode edits are automatically written **when returning to Play mode**,without Write. Powering off before returning loses those edits. Optional FS-5U at MANUAL REMOTE toggles Manual/Play. This differs from immediate continuous autosave. [Own manual p17][ME8-M] |

### ME-50B — synth and expression choices

Checked2026-10-18 against [original manual pp12–13,18–19][M50B-M]. Text inspection; no software/manual downloads or new visual control-count verification.

| Synth / waveform and controls | **SAW LEAD,MILD FILTER,DEEP FILTER use saw-wave synth sounds; SQUARE uses square-wave**. Allfour require single-note playing. DRIVE/SENS controls filtering; TONE/CUTOFF controls cutoff; D.LEVEL/RESONANCE controls resonance insynthmode,not dryblend. Do not transfer thedrive-mode direct-level meaning to synthmode. |
| Expression / six effect choices | **WAH,RESONANCE,+1 OCTAVE,−1 OCTAVE,SOUND HOLD,KICK DRUM**, alternatives tovolume mode. ±octave andSoundHold require single notes. RESONANCE cancombine withSAW LEAD/SQUARE; onepedaleffect isnot sixsimultaneousblocks. |
| Looper / Sound Hold distinction | SoundHold sustains aplayednote andpedalposition adjustsitslevel; raisingthetoe farenough cancelsit. It is distinctfrom2.8s HOLD DLY recording/layering,not anadditional general-purpose looper. |


| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Power / polarity evidence | Roland explicitly approves PSA 200/500mA adapters for ME-50B; its PSA-S specifications state **regulated 9V DC, center-negative, 500mA capacity**. Therefore center-negative compatibility is supported by the named approved supply, rather than sibling-model inference. Device draw remains140mA. PSA-S page lists100–240V50/60Hz input and warns regional versions/plugs differ; verify actual adapter label. | [ME-50B compatibility][M50B-PS]; [PSA-S specifications][M50B-PSAS] |


### ME-50B — startup and unsaved-edit limits

Checked2026-10-18 against the [exact original manual][M50B-M],printed pp6–8,22–23.

| Field | Operating qualification |
|---|---|
| Power / battery fallback | With batteries installed while using the adapter,the manual says operation continues if adapter power is interrupted. This is a fallback claim,not rechargeable-battery charging or a measured switchover-time guarantee. |
| Live / startup state | Power-up turns FILTER/TONE,DRIVE/SYNTH andDELAY/MOD off;expression starts asvolume. HOLD DLY instead entersrecord standby when selected. First entry intoMemory after power-up selects **bank1/patch1**. These states do not imply automatic restoration of the last live setup. |
| Memory / tuner discards temporary edits | P23 explicitly says temporary **tone edits andtempo settings are lost on patch change orentry toTuner**,unless written. Bank selection alone flashes the pending bank;the sound changes after selecting a number pedal. This is separate from the tuner-reference pitch saving behavior above. |


### ME-50B — compressor and FILTER/TONE inventory

Checked2026-10-18 against [original manual pp9–11][M50B-M].

| Field | Category choices / control limits |
|---|---|
| Processing / compressor choices | Five active choices: **NATURAL,LIMITER,160D SOFT,160D HARD,D-COMP**,plusOff. The shared depth knob controls **threshold for the first four,sustain forD-COMP**. The two160D settings represent lower/higher compression ratios;manual gives no numerical ratios here. These are alternatives,not five simultaneous dynamics blocks. |
| Processing / FILTER-TONE coverage | Eleven alternatives comprise three envelope-wah variants,two octave directions,volume swell,fretless simulation,enhancement,low boost,mid scoop andacoustic-electric bass body resonance. Onlyone is selected at atime. Wah variants differ in sweep direction/strength;the octave choices retain the previously documented single-note restriction. |
| Synth / fretless distinction | DEFRETTER explicitly simulates fretless-bass character from a conventional bass;HOLLOW instead adds acoustic-electric body resonance. Neither establishes general guitar-to-acoustic simulation,a sample library oruser IR loading. |


### ME-50B — tapped delay, saved level and recording standby

Checked 2026-10-18 against [original manual printed pp.7, 16–17][M50B-M]. Text inspection only; no unit operation.

| Field | Finding / qualification |
|---|---|
| Delay / tap versus saved range | ANALOG DLY, 0–400 ms DLY, 300–1400 ms DLY and CHO+DELAY support tapped intervals **62–1400 ms**. On Write, delay time is clamped to the selected type’s knob range: 600 ms in 0–400 mode saves as400; 200 ms in 300–1400 saves as300. This is distinct from dotted-eighth tap delay. |
| Effects / modulation tempo | PHASER, FLANGER and CHORUS support tapped cycles **62–2000 ms**. Moving TIME/RATE after tapping restores the knob-defined interval/rate. These are cycle periods, not delay lengths or a BPM specification. |
| Memory / master level | **MASTER LEVEL is saved per patch**. With all effects off, its midpoint provides matching input/output levels; excessive level can distort. Do not treat this knob as a necessarily global volume independent of patch recall. |
| Looper / preparation and quantization | HOLD DLY requires **2.8 seconds** to enter recording standby after selection or restart. With Kick Drum running, tap/record length is quantized and the kick is recorded into HOLD DLY; pedal movement cannot subsequently change that recorded kick level. |

<!-- comparison evidence ends -->

Searches for ME-50B, ME50B and ME 50B on 2026-10-17 did not resolve exact Shopee Indonesia hardware. Foreign accessory matches do not establish a hardware price or Indonesian stock. Seller/item, condition, variant, region, bundle, warranty, voucher and current availability remain unknown. This is a search limitation, not proof of market absence.

## ME-90

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. This is the guitar ME-90, not ME-90B. Original dates, sources and price qualifications remain below. Historical next-model notes are provenance; use research-state.json for current work.

<!-- BOSS-ME-90 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Updates an existing [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-026 --> record; not another model.

### Display and controls

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/me-90/) explicitly identifies a **two-character, seven-segment LED display**, not a graphical LCD. Graphical-screen diagonal/color classification is **N/A**; physical readout dimensions/color are not published in the checked specification. **No touchscreen interface** is documented. Multicolored footswitch status LEDs must not be described as a color graphical display.

The control list rechecks **25 knobs,8 main footswitches,4 buttons**, plus expression toe, rear power and output-mode switches separately. The page confirms36 factory+36 user presets,97 effect types,11 onboard amps,3 user IR slots and a38-second mono looper. Windows/macOS Tone Studio and IR loader remain separate applications; iOS/Android editing needs optional BT-DUAL. This is a scoped review, not a complete current algorithm-list transcription.

### Separate IDR evidence

|---|---:|---|
| Shopee retail, Eleven Music | **Rp7.450.000** | [shop12356205/item25352150117](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-ME-90-Guitar-Multi-Effects-Pedal-BOSS-ME90-Effect-Gitar-Original-i.12356205.25352150117); indexed hardware ask, detailed condition/checkout not verified |
| Indonesian retailer Swee Lee | **Rp7.633.000** | [Retail page](https://www.sweelee.co.id/en/products/boss-me-90-guitar-multi-effects-pedal), opened and displays price, tax inclusion and in-store/online availability; delivery terms remain subject to checkout |

Used-price leads: [OLX Studio/Panggung search](https://www.olx.co.id/bekasi-kota_g4000020/alat-musik-aksesoris_c217/q-boss?filter=type_eq_hobi-olahraga-alat-alat-musik-studio-panggung), [Bogor effects search](https://www.olx.co.id/bogor-kota_g4000021/alat-musik-aksesoris_c217/q-efek-bass). A category filter containing Bekas is not sufficient to assign an independently verified condition to an unopened individual ad. Neither lead is promoted to a confirmed active used lower bound.

The manufacturer includes batteries, not the optional PSA adapter. A retailer [explicitly lists the adapter as excluded](https://www.blibli.com/p/boss-me-90-guitar-multi-effects-pedal/ps--MEM-70158-00082). Another Shopee item mixes four variations and sold-out states; its whole range is not treated as the processor's market price. Seller authenticity, accessories, region and delivered cost remain unverified unless specifically stated.

Official product-image attempts failed; the segmented-display classification comes from the explicit specification, not an uninspected photo. Original USD observations remain historical. **Next existing-model review: GX-100.**

### ME-90 — original USD and technical provenance

Research date **2026-10-14**. Technical sources are the linked manufacturer's specifications, including current firmware-dependent figures. **Partial records**: `?` means unverified. Dimensions are **W × D × H in mm**; `/max` gives the maximum height with the pedal raised. `K/F/B` = rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons, excluding the expression toe switch and rear power/mode switches. Price columns are **USD US-market listed/asking observations**, not statistically estimated fair values; see sources below.

Model totals have different inclusion rules. ME-90's selectable alternatives are not all simultaneously present on its panel. A missing NAM claim is not converted into a definitive “No” without model/firmware documentation.

Price retrieval date: **2026-10-14**. Indexed older listings are explicitly less reliable than current, purchasable offers. No guarantee of stock, local Indonesian pricing, delivery cost or completed-sale value.

Eleven PEDAL FX choices retained; complete current category subtotals and explicit maximum simultaneous count need checking. [ME90-P] |

| Audio / control I/O | Mono quarter-inch INPUT; two quarter-inch outputs; mono SEND/RETURN;3.5 mm PHONES;USB-C; dedicated BT-DUAL connector;DC. No XLR DI or external CTL/EXP socket listed. [ME90-P] |

### ME-90B — external control and recording followup, 2026-10-18

| Field | Verified detail / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| External expression | No wired EXP socket in the published connector list. The manual explicitly supports optional EV-1-WL wireless expression and FS-1-WL wireless footswitches. The linked connection-guide page exposes a download agreement without a PDF in retrieved text; detailed wireless setup and firmware requirements remain unchecked. | [Reference manual][ME90B-M] p15; [EV-1-WL connection-guide landing page](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/me-90b/owners_manuals/77f6bba2-2c2d-4a68-87d9-582acd9c311d/) |
| USB audio / MIDI | Named audio endpoints: INPUT and MIX IN into the unit; OUTPUT and DRY OUT from it. These are endpoint names, not a verified channel count or bit depth. USB MIDI supports program changes and CC11 expression, CC82 pedal-effect on/off and CC80 CTL. Dedicated driver required. | [Reference manual][ME90B-M] pp12–13 |
| Routing / Bluetooth | Computer playback reaches main outputs, phones and balanced XLR; optional Bluetooth audio reaches main outputs/phones but **not balanced XLR**. USB loopback can return computer audio to USB OUT. | [Reference manual][ME90B-M] pp12,14 |

Offline liveset editing is documented, but saving edited memory results requires the unit connected. | [Tone Studio guide][ME90B-B] p1 |

| Memory / computer library | A liveset holds at most36 memories; Tone Studio holds up to50 livesets, with further sets exported to computer files. Liveset file extension **.tsl**. Export to the unit does not restore memories beyond36. These computer-library limits do not expand the pedal’s36user+36preset onboard memories. | [Tone Studio guide][ME90B-B] pp6–8 |

### ME-90B — model-specific support clarifications

Checked 2026-10-18 against manufacturer support articles updated 2026-05-01 and a separately identified Shopee hardware offer.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Power / voltage and adapter capacity | BOSS explicitly states **195 mA at 9 V DC**, compatible with older 200 mA PSA and later 500 mA PSA-S/PSA-S2 adapters. Four alkaline AA provide approximately six hours; batteries can remain installed as backup if the adapter disconnects. This article does not resolve USB power or directly specify socket polarity. | [Official ME-90B power article](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/24403972628251-ME-90B-What-kind-of-power-supplies-does-it-support) |
| Routing / output-select scope | OUTPUT SELECT applies only to quarter-inch OUTPUT jacks. Balanced XLR is fixed to LINE regardless of AMP/LINE switch or OUTPUT SELECT. | [Official output clarification](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/24404249160219-ME-90B-Which-outputs-does-the-OUTPUT-SELECT-setting-apply-to) |
| Looper / phrase storage | Phrases **cannot be saved inside ME-90B**. Manufacturer suggests recording via analog OUTPUT or USB audio, with the dedicated driver for computer recording. This is external audio recording, not onboard loop-file storage. | [Official phrase-storage answer](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/24403936681499-ME-90B-Can-I-save-a-phrase-created-with-the-Phrase-Loop-function-in-the-unit) |
| Memory / cross-model compatibility | Patch data is **not compatible with other ME-series units**. Shared product-family naming does not establish patch interchange. | [Official patch compatibility](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/24403944639643-ME-90B-Is-the-patch-data-compatible-between-ME-90B-and-other-ME-series) |

Late-delivery Rp10.000 voucher is compensation, not another price reduction. | [Exact Shopee offer](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-ME-90B-Bass-Multi-effects-Pedal-i.30194339.26223675342) |

### ME-90B — live memory and level behavior

Checked 2026-10-18, original reference manual revision01, printed pp2, 6–7, 16–17.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| I/O / output-volume scope | OUTPUT LEVEL controls main OUTPUT and PHONES volume, **not balanced XLR volume**. This is separate from the AMP/LINE and OUTPUT SELECT restrictions. | [ME90B-M] p2 |
| Memory / manual-mode transition | **N0** applies physical knob settings when entering manual mode; default **N1** retains memory-mode sound settings while allowing individual effects to be toggled. Manual settings are retained when returning to memory mode. | [ME90B-M] p6 |
| Memory / save behavior | Memory write saves sound settings **except OUTPUT LEVEL**. Unsaved edits are lost on power-off or a different memory selection. Writing can start in manual or memory mode; completion enters memory mode. | [ME90B-M] p7 |
| Routing / manual foot control | The manual-mode BLEND pedal can instead toggle SEND/RETURN using system option **r1**; default **r0** toggles BLEND. | [ME90B-M] p16 |
| Expression / reset exception | Factory reset excludes expression-pedal calibration while restoring user memories and other settings. | [ME90B-M] p17 |

| Processing / modulation choices | Guide p15: **12 top-level choices** per MOD1/MOD2: Chorus,2X2 Chorus,Phaser,Flanger,Bass Flanger,Tremolo,Pan,Rotary,Uni-V,Slicer,Vibrato,Ring Mod. Both blocks may select the same effect; subtype counts separate. [MS3-G] |
| Processing / delay choices | Guide pp19–22: **11**: Single,Pan,Stereo,Dual-S,Dual-P,Dual-L/R,Reverse,Analog,Tape,Modulate,Tera Echo. Dual algorithms remain one selected DLY type. [MS3-G] |
| Processing / reverb choices | Guide pp23–24: **8**: Ambience,Room,Hall1,Hall2,Plate,Spring,Modulate,Delay. [MS3-G] |
| Routing / limitations | Guide p4: L1–3 cannot move; FX1/FX2/MOD1/MOD2/DLY/REV/NS/FV can move around them. MST/PATCH LVL/OUTPUT/CTL cannot move. Mono processing or a loop after stereo processing loses that stereo effect. [MS3-G] |

### Nested choices audited from the original guide

| Field | Finding / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / nested choices | Guide pp8–10: WAH **6**, BASS WAH **6**, OD/DS **21**, BASS OD/DS **6** selectable types. Guide pp16–17: PHASER **4** (4/8/12-stage,BiPhase), PAN **2** (Auto,Manual). These are nested choices within the31 FX or12 MOD top-level selections, not extra simultaneous blocks. Their sum alone does not explain the advertised112. [MS3-G] |
| Processing / EQ conflict | Guide p6 describes GRAPHIC EQ as seven bands, while its p10 parameter table lists **10 frequencies,31Hz–16kHz**. Preserve this internal source conflict; do not silently replace either statement or count frequency bands as effect types. [MS3-G] |

### Effect-total arithmetic reconciliation

| Field | Finding / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / count accounting | Guide p14 SUB DELAY has **2 types,Mono/Pan**,1–1000ms; p15 Chorus has **3 output modes**, retained separately from effect-type counting. [MS3-G] |
| Processing / derived total | **Arithmetic inference**, not an explicit manufacturer counting formula: FX31 top-level choices, replacing7 grouped selectors by8 compressor+3 limiter+6 wah+6 bass wah+21 OD/DS+6 bass OD/DS+2 subdelay choices, gives **76**. MOD12, replacing Phaser/Pan by4/2, gives **16**. **76+16+11 DLY+8 REV+1 NS=112**. Shared FX1/FX2 and MOD1/MOD2 libraries are counted once; no duplicate count for block instances. Output modes and continuous parameters excluded. This matches the advertised total under that convention; it does not establish112 simultaneous effects or independent algorithms. [MS3-G], [MS3-P] |

These occupy the **same200 writable locations**, not200 user plus100 separate protected presets; write destinations01-1–50-4 overwrite existing data. Counts derived from listed bank ranges. [MS3-G] |

### MS-3 MIDI and performance-control limits

Checked 2026-10-18 using original owner/parameter-guide text only; no files downloaded or device operation.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| I/O / patch MIDI | Guide p39 lists PATCH MIDI 1–4, but its manual-transmit sentence says 1–8. Retain the internal conflict; eight slots are not established. Each listed setup has channel OFF/1–16, PC OFF/1–128 and two CC number/value pairs. Bank select cannot transmit alone, requires PC, and cannot enable only LSB. | [Parameter guide p39][MS3-G] |
| Synth / Sound Hold limitation | Sound Hold sustains a captured note while another melody is played; simultaneous multiple-note input is unsupported. Assign F1:SOL/TRG or F2:SOL/TRG to a controller. This is not evidence of phrase-loop recording, overdubbing or a synth engine. | [Parameter guide p6][MS3-G] |
| Controls / tuner outputs | Tuner reference 435–445 Hz. MUTE silences output; BYPASS disables effects; THRU retains the current effect sound while tuning. These are different output modes. | [Parameter guide p43][MS3-G] |
| Controls / lock and page buttons | Hold ON/OFF at least two seconds in the play screen to lock buttons and knobs; repeat to unlock. EDIT and ENTER double as page buttons, so page labels do not add two physical buttons to the existing five-button inventory. Hold MEMORY/MANUAL at least two seconds to enter MUTE & TUNER. | [Owner manual p5][MS3-M] |


### MS-3 corroborated MIDI setups and Tempo Hold

Checked 2026-10-18; documentation only, without installing the editor or testing a device.

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| I/O / MIDI setup count corroboration | **Four documented PATCH MIDI setups (1–4)**: Roland support updated 2024-12-18 uses that exact selection range; the original Japanese editor guide p5 says CTL OUT & MIDI SEND transmits the messages configured in PATCH MIDI 1–4. This corroborates the parameter-guide heading. Its conflicting 1–8 sentence remains preserved above, rather than establishing eight setups. Physical/firmware inspection was not performed. | [Roland support](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/360004192252-MS-3-MEMORY-MANUAL-MODE-CURRENT-NUMBER-MIDI); [Japanese editor guide p5](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/MS-3_Editor_Librarian_jpn02_W.pdf) |
| Controls / Tempo Hold | Set BPM preference to PATCH and TEMPO HOLD ON in the **destination patch**, then Write. The arriving patch retains the previous live BPM. Returning to a patch with Tempo Hold OFF restores that patch’s saved BPM; the guide illustrates 120→130→120. This is tempo persistence, not audio-tail continuation. | [Original application guide p14](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/MS-3_Application_Guide_eng01_W.pdf) |

**System target assignment supported:** [own parameter guide eng02][MS3-G],printed pp26,31,41: set **PREF EXP1/EXP2=SYSTEM**,thenEXP **FUNC=FV**,which controls Foot Volume LEVEL across patches. Do not select conditionalF1/F2:F/W/P for unconditionalvolume. FV can move in the patch chain;MIN/MAX0–100 andcurve controls are documented. Shared assignment doesnot prove globalFV placement/endpoints or immunity to competingpatchASSIGNs. Bypass,held-position recall,tail andrecorded-loop coverage remain unverified. | Category choices / delay3 | Multi Delay,Multi Tap Delay,Stereo Delay. |
| Category choices / other5 | Space Chorus,Multi Pitch Shifter,Stereo Pitch Shifter,Stereo Flanger,Stereo Phaser. |
| Category choices / multi9 | Vocoder,Rotary,Keyboard Multi1,Keyboard Multi2,Rhodes,Guitar Multi,Vocal Multi,Stereo Enhancer,2CH Mixer. |
| Category choices / paired4 | Reverb1+Reverb2,Gate Reverb+Reverb,Chorus+Reverb,Delay+Reverb. |

The contents andbody names reconcile28choices;OCR numbering mistakes are not promoted to preset mappings. Fixed combinations do not establish freely assignable blocks ormaximum concurrency. Earlier catalogue-transcription uncertainty remains.

### SE-50 — rear ports, backup battery and effects-off behavior

Checked2026-10-18 against the [French BOSS manual](https://fr.scribd.com/document/644989539/Boss-SE-50-fr),©1990,external descriptions,important notes andeffects on/off sections. Text inspection;connector diameters/electrical balancing remainunverified.

| Required field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| I/O / documented panel inventory | Audio INPUT L(MONO)/R andOUTPUT L(MONO)/R;MIDI IN/OUT;EFFECT REMOTE andNUMBER SHIFT switch jacks;AC-adapter connection. LEVEL selects nominal input level. Remote jacks are controls,not an audio send/return loop. Phones/USB are not listed in this inventory. |
| Power / backup battery distinction | Internal battery retains memory withpower off;manual recommends replacement everythree years,withactual life environment-dependent. This establishes **memory backup**,not battery-powered audio operation. MIDI backup orwritten settings are advised before possible memory loss. |
| Live / effects-off modes | **DIRECT** passes dry sound;**MUTE** suppresses output. Manual recommends MUTE for mixer send/return use. FS-5L atEFFECT REMOTE controls global effects on/off;whilethat footswitch controls it,thefrontEFFECT button is inactive. Analog true-bypass circuitry is not established. |

### SE-50 — vocoder and composite processing limits

Checked2026-10-18 against the [French manufacturer manual](https://fr.scribd.com/document/644989539/Boss-SE-50-fr),©1990,Vocoder,Rotary andKeyboard Multi1 sections. Extracted text ends before Guitar Multi;full chain topology remainsunverified.

| Required field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Synth / vocoder requirements | **Seven frequency bands** process external microphone audio against a sustained keyboard signal. Microphone uses **right input**;external preamplification is recommended. Super Chorus andNoise Suppressor are included. This is external-carrier vocoding,not proof of an onboard note-generating synthesizer. |
| Processing / Rotary controls | Separate low/high slow/fast rates andtransition times;Drive,level andseparation controls. **SPEED(TRIG)** toggles speed from a momentary MIDI controller;**SPEED** changes it while held. This does not establish an analog expression jack. |
| Processing / Keyboard Multi1 limits | EQ,delay,Super Chorus andreverb have documented parameters;delay **0–800ms**,reverb **0.1–20s**. Effects can be switched individually. These are within this algorithm,not a global freely assignable chain or28simultaneous effects. |

### SE-50 — input controls and patch-selection behavior

Checked2026-10-18 against [French manufacturer manual](https://fr.scribd.com/document/644989539/Boss-SE-50-fr),©1990,front-panel,input-level andprogram-selection sections. Text-derived;no visual control audit.

| Required field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Knobs / confirmed input controls | **Two independent rotary input-level controls**,left/right. Complete button/footswitch totals remainunverified. Overload indicators are documented **6dB below clipping**;they are not maximum-input specifications. |
| Live / patch stepping | One **FS-5U atNUMBER SHIFT** advances oneprogram perpress;holding does not auto-repeat. Two switches through **PCS-31** provide increase/decrease. A configurable range limits stepping;reversing its endpoints gives descending order. |
| Memory / power-on recall andediting | Power-on recalls the previously used program. Parameter edits are temporary untilwritten;changing programs orpowering off loses unsaved changes. This is distinct from automatic recall of an already stored program. |

### SE-50 — nominal sensitivity and global effects state

Checked2026-10-18 against the [French BOSS manual][SE50-FR], input-level and effects-on/off sections. Manufacturer-authored mirror text; no device or visual test.

| Required field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| I/O / nominal input sensitivity | Rear LEVEL selects **−20dBm or+4dBm**. If overload remains frequent at−20dBm even with the input controls at minimum, the manual directs selecting+4dBm. These are nominal sensitivity settings, **not maximum input ratings**; the separately documented overload warning remains6dB below clipping. |
| Live / global effects state | Programs can be selected while effects are off; the global on/off state is retained through other operations. This does not establish stored per-program bypass state, effect trails or power-cycle retention. |

### SE-50 — Keyboard Multi2 and algorithm-specific MIDI assignment

Checked2026-10-18 against the original1990 [French BOSS manual][SE50-FR],printed pp60–62,manufacturer-authored OCR reproduction;no visual or device test.

| Required field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / Keyboard Multi2 | Documents **phaser,EQ,Super Chorus and reverb**,with individual effect on/off. Phaser has four modes;chorus predelay0–60ms,reverb0.1–20s and reverb predelay0–200ms. These belong to this fixed algorithm,not freely assignable global blocks;diagram order and maximum whole-unit concurrency remain unverified. |
| Live / Keyboard Multi1 MIDI assignment | Its MIDI-control assignment selects **chorus rate,delay level,reverb level or master level**,with configurable minimum/maximum. Do not infer simultaneous control of all four or the same destinations in every algorithm. This is MIDI control,not proof of an analog expression input. |

### SE-50 — external expression through a MIDI controller

Checked2026-10-18 using the original©1991 BOSS FC-50 manual: [manufacturer PDF](https://cdn.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/FC-50_OM.pdf),indexed panel/power text,and [original manual OCR mirror](https://www.scribd.com/document/454763475/fc-50-manual-472118-pdf),introduction/connections. Direct PDF returns16pages with no extracted body;no visual audit.

| Required field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Expression / external MIDI path | FC-50 documentation explicitly names **SE-50** as a connected MIDI target. The controller accepts **two external expression pedals**,specifies **Roland EV-5 or BOSS EV-10**,and sends real-time Control Change messages. This documents an indirect pedal→controller→MIDI path;pedal sockets belong to FC-50,not SE-50. Exact CC mapping and target assignment must match the SE-50 algorithm;two independently controllable SE-50 destinations are not established. Direct analog EXP and toe-switch support remain unverified. |
| Live / controller MIDI channel | FC-50 defaults to channel1;manual explicitly tells SE-50 users to set its MIDI channel to1 for that setup. This is a controller setup requirement,not proof that SE-50 is restricted to channel1. |

### SE-50 — stereo pitch and gated flanger behavior

Checked2026-10-18 against the original©1990 [French BOSS manual][SE50-FR],Stereo Pitch Shifter and Stereo Flanger sections,printed pp48–51. OCR text only;no audio or visual test.

| Required field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Processing / Stereo Pitch Shifter | Per-channel **−12 to+12 semitones**,fine tuning,and **0–600ms predelay**;feedback and cross-feedback,independent wet/dry levels. **STEREO LINK** synchronizes channels and is required for MIDI fine-pitch control to affect both. Zero predelay can still have processing delay. These are this algorithm’s limits;polyphonic tracking and zero-latency operation are not established. |
| Processing / Stereo Flanger | **Two flanger modes**,modulation phase **0–180°**,plus periodically chopping **Gate** with on/off and rate. The gate here is not merely the separately named Noise Suppressor. |
| Live / flanger MIDI gate | **GATE ON/OFF(TRIG)** toggles from a momentary MIDI control;**GATE ON/OFF** changes state while held. Other destinations include flanger rate,gate rate and master level. These are algorithm-specific assignments,not direct analog pedal sockets. |

### SE-50 — direct expression capability screen

Checked2026-10-28 against the original©1990 [French BOSS manual][SE50-FR],external descriptions andfoot-control sections. Complete rear inventory has no analog EXP input: EFFECT REMOTE accepts a bypass switch;NUMBER SHIFT accepts program-step switches. This resolves the earlier direct-input uncertainty for that documented inventory. FC-50 expression-to-MIDI remains an indirect path,not a processor EXP socket.

Guitar/bass connection is explicit. No dedicated headphone socket is listed;front NUMBER controls are hand buttons,not onboard footswitches. Direct analog volume assignment/range/recall are inapplicable. MIDI-volume placement,endpoints,recall,bypass/tail/loop attenuation andchassis fit remain unverified. DIRECT/MUTE effects-off choices are not continuous pedal-volume control. No SE-70 orSE-50A dimensions transferred.

The manual’s external-switch table separately calls its target correction amount; retain both descriptions rather than asserting a calibrated wet/dry control. Enhance has OFF, LIGHT (green) and BRIGHT (red), two active alternatives within one stage. | [Own Spanish manual, panel descriptions][VE1-M] |

Blinking after editing an existing memory indicates changed settings that need saving; selecting a memory is not itself a save. Normal/manual remains separate from the three stored memories. | [Own Spanish manual, MEMORY section][VE1-M] |

PitchCorrect5types,Tone/SFX6types,reverb5types;delay1–4000ms. HarmonybuttonCUSTOM togglesmultiplechosencategories. | [Product][VE5-P];[Officialmanual pp16–17][VE5-PDF] |

Scale offers Chromatic or Maj(Min); Robot uses a fixed Note setting. Scale, Gender and Octave are parameters, not extra effect engines. Checked2026-10-18 from indexed own manual p16; not borrowed from VE-20’s four-type list. | [Own manual][VE5-PDF] |
| Category choices / named Tone-SFX modes | **PREAMP, DISTORTION, RADIO, STROBE, CHORUS, FLANGER**: six alternatives. PREAMP supplies gain/EQ controls; DISTORTION has Drive/Tone; STROBE and FLANGER have Speed; CHORUS has Depth. These conditional parameters do not mean all six types run together. Checked2026-10-18 from indexed own manual p16. | [Own manual][VE5-PDF] |
| Category choices / named reverb modes | **Ambience, Room, Hall1, Hall2, Plate**: five alternatives; Size changes reverb duration and RevLevel its amount. Verified2026-10-18 in manufacturer-authored2012 manual p17 mirrored at Scribd; original oversized PDF remains inaccessible in full. | [Manufacturer manual copy][VE5-COPY] |
| Harmony / type range and conditional controls | Manufacturer manual p17 lists **Double, key-based interval harmony from −1 to +1 octave, and Unison**. It does not enumerate every intermediate interval here; do not infer a chromatic semitone menu or count each interval as another engine. Double has **Light/Normal/Deep** depth. HrmLevel, Gender, Key and DirectLevel apply only to interval/Unison modes; Double depth is separate. Checked2026-10-18 from the identity-matched2012 manual copy. | [Manufacturer manual copy][VE5-COPY] |
| Harmony / part evidence | Own manual p6 describes **one added harmony part** above the singer. Do not borrow VE-20’s two-part architecture or VE-500’s three-part architecture. Manual p7 demonstrates changing the interval from third to fourth and independently adjusting harmony Gender. Full table’s −1 to +1 octave range is recorded above; intermediate menu steps remain unenumerated. | [Manufacturer manual copy pp6–7][VE5-COPY] |
| Power / adapter change interruption | Own manual p3 instructs disconnecting the adapter for battery operation and says connecting/disconnecting adapter or its mains lead **while powered on turns the unit off even with batteries installed**; unsaved data can be lost. Battery backup therefore does not provide documented uninterrupted adapter hot-swap. This resolves the earlier hot-swap unknown. | [Manufacturer manual copy p3][VE5-COPY] |
| Factory / user memory | **50soundlocations;01–30factory-filled**. ThreeFAVORITEA–Crecallassignments,not3additionalpatches. | [Product][VE5-P];[Originalmanual p13 mirrored][VE5-COPY] |

### VE-5 — original manual electrical and memory distinctions

Checked2026-10-18 against the [manufacturer-authored2012 VE-5 manual copy][VE5-COPY],printed pp16–18,20. Exact VE-5 identity,controls andcopyright retained;original document body only,no generated summary.

This independently corroborates the historical catalogue figures without relying onits dense multi-model extraction. Current manufacturer product's **TBD/66Ω** remains aconflict;no hardware revision oruniversal replacement value established. |
| Memory / copy,exchange andfactory recovery | **COPY TO overwrites destination**;EXCHANGE swaps two sound locations. **COPY PRESET** loads afactory preset into the currently selected sound,distinct from resetting allsounds/system. Names support up to12characters. These operations do not add slots orpreserve overwritten custom settings automatically. Manual p18. |
| Processing / dynamics parameter count | DYNAMICS has Depth andEnhance controls;Enhance increases definition. These are two parameters within thecategory,not two newly counted effects. MASTER NoiseThres is a separate sound-level noise adjustment,not a documented second dynamics engine. Manual pp16–17. |

## VE-8

### VE-8 — preserved original comparison

Checked **2026-10-14**. **Partial records**: `?` means unverified, not No. Dimensions are **W×D×H, mm**. K/F/B = rotary controls / main footswitches / separate buttons; rear power, phantom and ground-lift toggles are additional. Used and new prices are separate **USD US-market asking/listing observations**, not sold-price statistics. Tax, shipping and condition matter.

A guitar input used to detect chords does not automatically provide guitar effects. VE-500's instrument THRU sends the **unprocessed guitar signal** to other pedals. VE-8, by contrast, has its own acoustic-guitar processing path.

### VE-8 — CHORUS concurrency and effect settings

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |

| Processing / guitar section concurrency | **Up to three CHORUS-section effects simultaneously**, selecting one from each group. Eight choices: Chorus, Tremolo, Phaser, Delay, Mod Delay, Octave, Slow Gear, Ring Mod. This corrects any reading of the earlier “alternatives” wording as strictly one-at-a-time. Extracted group diagram does not reliably preserve group boundaries; exact grouping remains unverified. | [Manual pp8–9][VE8-M] |

| Processing / reverb and pitch correction | Guitar and vocal reverb choices: **Ambience, Room, Hall1, Hall2**. Vocal correction: Off, Soft, Hard. Guitar tuner reference435–445Hz, default440; holding CHORUS2s mutes guitar and enters tuner. | [Manual pp8,10][VE8-M] |

### VE-22 — output and loop operation,2026-10-17

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| I/O / output modes | **MONO** sums internal stereo to both XLR outputs;**STEREO** preserves L/R;**FX/DRY** sends mono dry+effect mix to L and bypass to R. The left side is not isolated wet-only audio. OUTPUT settings apply globally and save automatically. | [Reference p21][VE22-M] |
| Looper / recording and control | Recorded phrases **cannot be saved** in device memory. PERFORM records harmony/effect/echo;CHECK records before those processors for sound adjustment. Built-in loop control requires Memory mode;Manual mode needs external switches assigned LOOP/LOOP CTL. Double-tap within one second stops;hold clears. | [Reference pp22–23][VE22-M] |
| Expression / memory behavior | **EXP HOLD** globally retains the previous memory’s pedal value when enabled;automatically saved. Unlike that system setting,edited sound parameters require MEMORY WRITE before memory change or power-off. | [Reference pp8,21][VE22-M] |

| Physical controls / panel lock | Long-press EXIT on the play screen locks MEMORY/HARMONY/EFFECT/ECHO knobs and MENU; long-press again unlocks. The documented lock scope does not include PHONES/LINE or MIC SENS. | [VE22-M] p2 |

## VE-500

Vocal multi-effects with guitar/MIDI-guided harmony and vocoder. Consolidated/researched2026-10-16;partial verification.

### VE-500 — preserved original comparison

Checked **2026-10-14**. **Partial records**: `?` means unverified, not No. Dimensions are **W×D×H, mm**. K/F/B = rotary controls / main footswitches / separate buttons; rear power, phantom and ground-lift toggles are additional. Used and new prices are separate **USD US-market asking/listing observations**, not sold-price statistics. Tax, shipping and condition matter.

A guitar input used to detect chords does not automatically provide guitar effects. VE-500's instrument THRU sends the **unprocessed guitar signal** to other pedals. VE-8, by contrast, has its own acoustic-guitar processing path.

### VE-500 — manufacturer checks

| Required field | Evidence / limits (2026-10-16) |
|---|---|
| Physical controls / expression | Three assignable knobs,three footswitches,five buttons per manufacturer control list;rear ground-lift separate. No builtin expression/toe pedal;TRS external EXP or two footswitches. [VE500-S] |
| Display / body / memory |132×32 backlit graphic LCD;diagonal/color/touch unestablished.170×138×62mm,860g.50preset+99user=149 patches total. [VE500-S] |
| Processing / choices | Up to nine simultaneous effects. [VE500-S] Four independent FX engines each offer20 effect types;same type can occupy multiple FX engines. Two independent reverb/delay units. Pitch correction available only in HRM mode;HRM/VOC are alternatives. [VE500-M] Remaining subtype counts unverified. |
| Routing / bypass and output-mode scope | Roland’s own Japanese support FAQs (updated **2023-11-20**, checked **2026-10-18**) confirm **bypass audio also undergoes A/D and D/A conversion**; DRY audio passes through ENHANCE (preamp). **OUTPUT L/R mode cannot be set independently per patch.** These statements concern vocal output routing and do not change the documented unbuffered instrument THRU. [Bypass FAQ](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404893737627); [Output-mode FAQ](https://support.roland.com/hc/ja/articles/4404893738011) |
| Routing / live | Four FX structures:SERIES,PARA+SER,2PARALLEL,4PARALLEL. Reverb structures:SERIES,PARALLEL,SEPARATE. Tuner sources MIC,INST or both;reference435–445Hz. Vocal tuner output can BYPASS,DRY(Enhance),LEAD(Enhance+Pitch Correct),orMUTE. [VE500-M] |
| Looper | **60s mono/30s stereo**;content lost on patch change,loop-mode change or poweroff. Assign loop functions to controllers;one-shot,overdub,fade and playback-decay settings. [VE500-P] |
| Ports / power / guitar | XLR mic48V10mAmax;quarter-inch instrument in/unbuffered THRU;stereo XLR outputs with selectable-40/-10dBu;TRS CTL/EXP;DIN MIDI IN;micro-B USB;DC.310mA phantom-on/210mA off;PSA supplied. No headphone connector listed. **USB audio is confirmed by the own manuals below**; bus power and battery operation remain unverified. **9 V DC center-negative** is resolved through the explicitly specified PSA supply and manufacturer adapter documentation below. [VE500-S] |
| Power / approved-supply polarity | **9 V regulated DC, center-negative**, established by VE-500’s explicit PSA-series requirement plus Roland’s PSA-S specification (updated June16,2026). PSA-S **500 mA is adapter capacity**, distinct from the VE-500’s **310 mA with phantom /210 mA without** draw. Actual seller-supplied adapter, regional plug and input label still need confirmation. |
| USB audio / confirmed functions | **USB audio and MIDI supported**, explicitly stated in owner manual p3. Parameter Guide p30 provides **IN LEVEL and OUT LEVEL 0–200**, computer audio routing **MIX** (internal mixer) or **PRE FX** (Input EQ then effects), and **LOOP BACK** to return computer audio mixed with VE-500 audio to the computer. LOOP BACK ON disables ROUTING selection. Earlier “USB audio not established” observations above are superseded by this2026-10-18 manual check; channel count and audio format remain unverified. [VE500-M];[VE500-P] |
| USB audio / monitoring persistence | DIRECT MON controls the direct VE-500 signal at the outputs. It **cannot be saved and resets ON at power-up**. OFF leaves USB-return audio at the outputs and requires computer audio-through for monitoring the processed input. Avoid treating OFF as a persistent stand-alone mute setting. [VE500-P p30] |
| Power / DC switching evidence | Owner manual p8 says inserting the adapter into DC IN powers the unit on and disconnecting it powers it off. Its supply table lists AC adapter; no explicit battery or USB-bus-power capability was located. Those capabilities remain unverified rather than inferred from the USB connector or audio support. [VE500-M pp8,19] |
| Editor / MIDI | Windows/Mac full editor/librarian;USB-MIDI and DIN MIDI can guide harmony/pitch. [VE500-S]USB driver required by owner manual;patch edit/name/reorder/backup distinct from firmware. [VE500-M] Initial2026-10-16 OS gap;see separately dated2026-10-17 compatibility rows below. |
| Synth / acoustic / loading | Guitar input can excite vocoder/talkbox sound;THRU remains unprocessed. [VE500-S] This does not establish guitar amp/acoustic multifx,IR,native NAM,converted NAM or capture loading. |
| Category choices / Enhance and pitch correction | ENHANCE combines definition, compression, de-essing and EQ controls; these are not alternative block types. PITCH CORRECT has **four types: SOFT, HARD, ELECTRIC, ROBOT**, and CHROMATIC/KEY scale settings. [VE500-P p5] |
| Category choices / harmony parts | **Three harmony parts**, each with voice, pan, level and character controls. Four user-interval maps are settings, not four extra simultaneous voices. Selecting USR-INT disables MIDI/INST chord detection. Double has LIGHT/NORMAL/DEEP intensity plus OFF. [VE500-P pp6–7] |
| Category choices / vocoder | **Three types: STANDARD, VINTAGE, TALK BOX**. Sources: INST, MIDI, MIDI(USB), MIC, AUTO. INST carrier has six choices (SAW, VINTAGE SAW, DETUNE SAW, SQUARE, RECTANGLE, DISTORTION); non-INST replaces DISTORTION with SUPER SAW. Source/carrier choices are parameters, not additional simultaneous engines. HARMONY and VOCODER remain alternatives. [VE500-P pp6–7] |
| Categories / FX1–4 inventory | **20 alternatives per engine**:Distortion,Radio,Lo-Fi,Filter,T.Wah,Ring Modulator,Chorus,Flanger,Tremolo,Phaser,Rotary,Slicer,Isolator,Vibrato,Pan,Roll,Freeze,Granular Delay,Delay,Reverb. Same type can occupy multiple engines. These are choices,not80 simultaneous effects. [VE500-P p8] |
| Categories / reverb engine distinction | FX Reverb has **5 types**:Ambience,Room,Hall,Plate,Mod;0.1–10s,pre-delay0–100ms. Each dedicated REV1/REV2 has those5 **plusDelay=6 choices**. Its reverb pre-delay1 is0–200ms,pre-delay2Off/0–500ms;Delay1–2000ms/BPM. Do not copy dedicated-REV ranges intoFX Reverb. [VE500-P pp13–14] |
| Looper / location | **8 positions**:MICIN,PRE FX1/2/3/4,PRE REV1/2,OUTPUT. Position changes where loop audio is captured relative to processing;not8 independent loopers. [VE500-P p17] |
| Editor / Windows qualification | Editor **1.00** page listsWindows10,8.1,7SP1+;requires productdriver. Separate driver **1.0.1 (Dec2021)** supportsWindows11/10x64;Win10x86 remains1.0.0. Driver excludesARM PCs,virtualWindows,Windows onMac and10Mobile. Driver11support alone does not certify editor11support. [VE500-EW];[VE500-DW] |
| Editor / current macOS matrix | Checked2026-10-17,matrixupdatedSep16 2026:VE500 **Hardware&Driver** and **ExclusiveApplication** bothYes(Download) for15/26;27UnderTesting. Editor1.00 requires driver;Mac driver1.0.3 is listed separately. This is dedicated-software support,not class-compliant/no-driver proof. [VE500-C];[VE500-EM];[VE500-DM] |
| Firmware / live qualifications | OwnSystemProgram1.10(Jan2025)changesAutoOff optionsOff/20min/10h,default20min. 1.07(Dec2021)fixesLEADFX withLOOP MICIN and permits knob patchselection beyondPATCH EXTENT;1.05(Jan2019)fixes some editor parameter settings;1.04(Nov2018)fixes externalMIDI withoutactive-sensing problems. Seller installedversion unknown;not claims thatcurrentfirmware retains oldbugs. [VE500-F] |

### VE-500 — current Windows application qualification

Checked 2026-10-18.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| Editor / Windows11 application support | [BOSS Windows matrix, updated2026-10-01](https://www.boss.info/global/support/support_news/25090811win1/) lists VE-500 **Hardware & Driver** and **Exclusive Application** separately as **Yes(Download)** for Windows11 and Windows10. This resolves the earlier editor-Windows11 gap using application-specific evidence, not the driver listing alone. Matrix legend excludes ARM PCs, virtual Windows and Windows running on Mac. Windows10 Yes entries carry an end-of-support caveat: operation is not guaranteed regardless of table status. Published compatibility is not a local operational test or certification of every Windows11 update. Earlier editor1.00 requirements remain as dated provenance. |

### VE-500 — MIDI visibility, reset scope and guitar detection

Checked 2026-10-18 in Roland’s own support articles.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| MIDI / harmony menu visibility | **M>VOICE**, HRM1–3 page5, appears only when a MIDI device is connected through the **5-pin MIDI IN** connector. Missing page5 is therefore not proof that the parameter is absent. This specific condition does not establish equivalent USB-only visibility. [Roland article, updated2026-05-04](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/360016423292-VE-500-MIDI-to-Voice-Parameters-Not-Visible-in-HRM-Effect) |
| Presets / selective reset | Factory Reset FROM/TO can select **SYSTEM or U01–U99**. SYSTEM-only or a selected user-patch range can be reset; affected settings are lost. This is not evidence of additional memories or a stored-loop backup. [Roland article, updated2026-07-02](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/360015997611-VE-500-How-do-I-restore-the-factory-settings-Factory-Reset) |
| Routing / chord detection | Roland recommends guitar → VE-500 INST, then unprocessed THRU → external guitar processor. Sending an already effected guitar into VE-500 is discouraged because dry guitar gives better chord recognition. This does not add guitar processing to the vocal unit. [Roland connection guidance](https://support.roland.com/hc/en-us/articles/17572594030619-How-to-connect-a-BOSS-GT-001-to-a-VE-500-or-VE-8) |

### VE-500 — processing ratings are separate from USB format

Checked 2026-10-18.

| Field | Evidence / qualification |
|---|---|
| I/O / converter and processing ratings | Own specification: **48 kHz sampling**, **32-bit AD + AF method**, **32-bit DA**, **32-bit floating-point processing**. These fields do not specify USB transfer bit depth or channel count. [Manufacturer specifications](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ve-500/specifications/) |
| I/O / analog electrical ratings | MIC **−40 dBu adjustable,4 kΩ**; INST **−10 dBu,2 MΩ**; unbuffered THRU **−10 dBu**. XLR L/R selectable **−40/−10 dBu**, **600 Ω output impedance**. Output impedance is not recommended load. [Manufacturer specifications](https://www.boss.info/global/products/ve-500/specifications/) |
| Software / automatic driver installation | Windows10/11 can automatically obtain the dedicated VE-500 driver from the Internet when connected. This does **not** mean class-compliant/no-driver operation. Public page points to archive/installed readmes for additional instructions but does not publish USB audio channel count or format; no archive downloaded. [Exact driver page](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/ve-500/updates_drivers/8efe83ff-2db5-4335-9e8b-33ee8de756ae/) |
